{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"Design a \"peak-innovative\" workstream for SIGIL (a local-first, offense-free, provable personal AI orchestrator at /home/kali/sigil): a FAST, low-latency HAND-GESTURE control system — a device camera → on-box deep-learning hand tracking → gesture intent → control the cursor/apps/keyboard — PC-first (Win/macOS/Linux) with a phone companion. The user wants \"the most advanced hand gestures possible, built from foundation, deep-learning integration.\" Stay strictly inside doctrine: every action carries proof of authorization (WARDEN tiers A0/A1/A2/A3, fail-closed); append-only signed spine; local-first; prove-don't-guess; OFFENSE-FREE (assert_no_offense guards imports; observe the owner's OWN hand + control the owner's OWN device only). Phase cadence: reuse-first → deterministic tests → real proof → red-pen adversarial review → fix → PR.\n\nSUBSTRATE (use this; do NOT re-explore):\n- sigil/perception/capture.py: Frame(kind,sha256,text,image_path) + grab_camera(device=\"/dev/video0\") is SINGLE-SHOT (cold ffmpeg per frame, timeout=20) — NO streaming/FPS posture; StaticFrame is the deterministic double. Needs a persistent streaming source.\n- REAL-TIME LOOP PRECEDENT: sigil/voice/backends.py MicAudioSource.frames() is a generator yielding fixed-size frames from a live device stream; sigil/voice/pipeline.py VoicePipeline is a frame-driven FSM (states IDLE/LISTENING/…, on_frame(frame) = one tick, timing in FRAME COUNTS not wallclock, barge-in with HYSTERESIS = barge_in_frames consecutive frames confirm — the exact debounce a gesture needs so one noisy frame can't fire a click); run.py run_mic is the live daemon loop with graceful model fallback.\n- MODEL PROVIDER PATTERN: sigil/perception/vision.py VisionModel Protocol carries `egresses: bool` (on-box=False runs A0; cloud=True is structurally refused on the auto path); MoondreamVision (local Ollama) / ClaudeVision (frontier); consolidate/extract.py ExtractionProvider (Agent/Api/Local/Replay/Heuristic doubles). Mirror this: a GestureModel Protocol + lazy-imported real provider + a zero-dep deterministic scripted double.\n- onnxruntime==1.27.0 is ALREADY vendored (usable for an on-box hand-landmark model). mediapipe/opencv/pyautogui/pynput/uinput are ABSENT (lazy-import behind a provider, per the voice ML convention — heavy deps stay out of requirements.txt).\n- CROSS-PLATFORM: sigil/platform/base.py HostBackend Protocol (capture_screen/camera/capabilities) + CapabilityDescriptor(host_id,os,has_screen,has_camera,has_gpu_vlm,always_on); __init__.host() selects by sys.platform; linux/macos/windows backends. Capabilities are advertised OWNER-SIGNED via sigil/mesh/registry.py (_CAP_CORE signed tuple — a new capability flag must be added to the signed core). NO input-injection code exists anywhere (greenfield).\n- WARDEN TIERING: sigil/agents/kernel_classify.py KernelClassifier.classify(tool)->Tier shells kernel/src/tiers.rs (token-based, danger-first, FAIL-CLOSED A3). CRITICAL FACT: every obvious input tool token (cursor.move, input.click, input.type, pointer.move, input.tap/swipe/scroll) classifies FAIL-CLOSED to A3 today (not in any allowlist). The Operator (sigil/agents/operator.py) is the derive-tier→preview→approval-gate→execute→verify precedent. mesh authorized_devices/DeviceApprover lets a phone approve by signing.\n\nDesign a DETAILED plan. Produce:\n1. PEAK-INNOVATIVE mechanisms — genuinely advanced, not just \"wire mediapipe to pyautogui\": (a) a persistent low-latency camera STREAM (upgrade the single-shot grab to a MicAudioSource-style generator); (b) DEEP-LEARNING from foundation — a hand-LANDMARK model (21 keypoints, on-box via onnxruntime, lazy provider + scripted double) FEEDING a trained GESTURE CLASSIFIER over landmark sequences (static poses: point/pinch/fist/open-palm; dynamic: swipe/circle/drag) — design the model/feature pipeline + how it's trained/shipped on-box; (c) a gesture-intent FSM mirroring VoicePipeline with HYSTERESIS/debounce so an ambiguous or noisy gesture does NOTHING (fail-safe); (d) the KEYSTONE SECURITY design: an OWNER-ARMED gesture SESSION (opt-in, indicator-lit, like ambient vision) that grants a bounded auto-tier ONLY for reversible pointer MOVEMENT within the session, while discrete ACTION gestures (click/type/drag/app-launch) derive their tier from the WARDEN oracle and gate — requiring a DELIBERATE, reviewed extension of tiers.rs to add input tokens at correct tiers (pointer.move=A0/A1 session-bounded, click=A1, type/scroll=A1/A2, launch=A2); this is a security-critical WARDEN change — design it so a gesture can NEVER trigger an unintended A2/A3 action; (e) per-OS input-injection backends (Linux uinput/ydotool, macOS CGEvent, Windows SendInput) + camera streaming, slotted into HostBackend + new signed CapabilityDescriptor flags.\n2. Ordered, independently-mergeable SLICES naming files to create/modify + exact reuse points.\n3. Test + real-proof strategy per slice (deterministic scripted-landmark doubles; a real webcam proof gated skip-if-absent) + the red-pen lenses (esp: can a gesture fire an unintended gated action? is pointer-move truly bounded to the armed session? does the oracle extension weaken any existing gate? latency honesty; injection can't run outside an armed session).\n4. Doctrine-safety analysis (offense-free, fail-closed, owner-armed, WARDEN-gated input) + abuse vectors a reviewer will hunt.\n5. Speed/latency design (target ms, on-box inference, frame-count FSM) and how \"fast\" is proven.\nDo not write code; design + enumerate. Return a thorough structured document."},"uuid":"e9f62a40-a770-4927-a19e-cdb0a8c5ef41","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:29:04.651Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"b2605094-9af5-487e-9616-573edf66748c","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- crucible: How to operate CRUCIBLE — the authorized, prove-don't-guess offensive-security engine in framework/v2. Read this BEFORE running an engagement/scan, re-verifying a finding, inspecting the event spine, reasoning about the veracity/anti-hallucination layer, the learning/metacognition core, or the safety stack. Use it when a task names CRUCIBLE, OBSIDIAN, `python3 -m framework.v2 <subcommand>`, findings/oracles/evidence certificates, the blackboard event spine, critics, reflection, or the reward/calibration loop.\n- deep-research: Deep research harness — fan-out web searches, fetch sources, adversarially verify claims, synthesize a cited report. - When the user wants a deep, multi-source, fact-checked research report on any topic. BEFORE invoking, check if the question is specific enough to research directly — if underspecified (e.g., \"what car to buy\" without budget/use-case/region), ask 2-3 clarifying questions to narrow scope. Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- adversarial-sweep: Independent multi-lens adversarial re-attack of a defensive-oracle domain — the FORGE stage-9 companion to RED-PEN. Attack from disjoint lenses, refute each objection with a real repro, report only what survives. - Before merging any AEGIS/FORGE domain stream, alongside (not instead of) the red-pen agent. Pass the domain spec via args. On the email-auth stream it caught 3 CRITICAL/LOW defects PAST a RED-PEN PASS — near-zero-FP cannot be self-certified by one reviewer.\n- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".\n- artifact-design: Design guidance and fundamentals for Artifacts.\n- artifact-capabilities: Runtime capabilities a published Artifact page can be granted — behavior static HTML cannot provide on its own, such as the page reading live or connected data, keeping state shared across viewers, or updating and republishing itself. Serves this user's live capability roster and the typed call definitions. Load it whenever the user asks for an artifact needing any such runtime behavior.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by exercising it end-to-end and observing behavior — drive the affected flow, not just tests or typecheck. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. Don't invoke it on a diff that only touches tests, docs, or other code with no runtime surface to drive (a change to product source always has one) — there's nothing to observe.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled cloud agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring cloud agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. {"parentUuid":"fb94dc00-73db-4fb6-903e-59905e762c18","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013Wik8u1pTMPfomKhV4PH3p","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The SIGIL voice pipeline (SIGIL §8) — a frame-driven, full-duplex state machine.\n2\t\n3\t    IDLE ──wake word──▶ LISTENING ──end-of-speech──▶ THINKING ──▶ SPEAKING ──done──▶ IDLE\n4\t      ▲                    │                                          │\n5\t      └────timeout─────────┘                       barge-in (VAD)─────┘  (cancel TTS, re-LISTEN)\n6\t\n7\tFull-duplex + barge-in are the acceptance bar (§8): while SPEAKING, every input frame is\n8\tVAD-checked, and `barge_in_frames` CONSECUTIVE speech frames (hysteresis) cancel the TTS and\n9\tre-enter LISTENING. The machine is driven one input frame at a time and holds no audio/ML deps\n10\titself (those are the injected components), so the whole behaviour is deterministically testable.\n11\t\n12\tLIVE-PATH NOTE: on a real mic+speaker the assistant's own TTS is captured by the mic, so\n13\tbarge-in needs acoustic-echo cancellation (AEC) or output ducking; without it, run the live\n14\tloop half-duplex (gate the mic while SPEAKING). Hysteresis alone does not defeat sustained\n15\tself-echo — AEC is the real fix and is a runtime/hardware concern, not a state-machine one.\"\"\"\n16\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n17\t\n18\tfrom enum import Enum\n19\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n20\t\n21\tfrom .components import Asr, AudioSink, Dispatch, Tts, Vad, WakeWord\n22\t\n23\t\n24\tclass State(Enum):\n25\t    IDLE = \"idle\"\n26\t    LISTENING = \"listening\"\n27\t    THINKING = \"thinking\"\n28\t    SPEAKING = \"speaking\"\n29\t\n30\t\n31\tclass VoicePipeline:\n32\t    def __init__(self, vad: Vad, wake: WakeWord, asr: Asr, tts: Tts, sink: AudioSink, dispatch: Dispatch,\n33\t                 *, silence_frames: int = 25, min_speech_frames: int = 3, listen_timeout_frames: int = 150,\n34\t                 max_utterance_frames: int = 1000, barge_in_frames: int = 4):\n35\t        self.vad, self.wake, self.asr, self.tts, self.sink, self.dispatch = vad, wake, asr, tts, sink, dispatch\n36\t        self.silence_frames = silence_frames          # ~500 ms of trailing silence ends the utterance\n37\t        self.min_speech_frames = min_speech_frames     # require real speech before ending\n38\t        self.listen_timeout_frames = listen_timeout_frames  # give up if no speech after wake (~3 s)\n39\t        self.max_utterance_frames = max_utterance_frames    # absolute cap so LISTENING always terminates (~20 s)\n40\t        self.barge_in_frames = barge_in_frames         # consecutive speech frames to CONFIRM a barge-in (hysteresis)\n41\t\n42\t        self.state = State.IDLE\n43\t        self.transcript: Optional[str] = None\n44\t        self.response: Optional[str] = None\n45\t        self.events: List[str] = []\n46\t        self._captured: List = []\n47\t        self._silence = 0\n48\t        self._speech = 0\n49\t        self._listen = 0\n50\t        self._tts_iter = None\n51\t        self._barge = 0\n52\t        self._barge_buf: List = []\n53\t\n54\t    # --- one input frame drives the machine --------------------------------------------------\n55\t    def on_frame(self, frame) -> None:\n56\t        if self.state is State.IDLE:\n57\t            if self.wake.detect(frame):\n58\t                self._to_listening([])\n59\t        elif self.state is State.LISTENING:\n60\t            self._listening(frame)\n61\t        elif self.state is State.SPEAKING:\n62\t            self._speaking(frame)\n63\t        # THINKING is transient (handled synchronously on entry); it never awaits input frames.\n64\t\n65\t    def _listening(self, frame) -> None:\n66\t        self._captured.append(frame)\n67\t        self._listen += 1\n68\t        if self.vad.is_speech(frame):\n69\t            self._speech += 1\n70\t            self._silence = 0\n71\t        else:\n72\t            self._silence += 1\n73\t        if self._speech >= self.min_speech_frames and self._silence >= self.silence_frames:\n74\t            self._to_thinking()\n75\t        elif self._listen >= self.max_utterance_frames:\n76\t            # absolute cap — LISTENING can never wedge (e.g. VAD stuck-high on ambient noise)\n77\t            if self._speech >= self.min_speech_frames:\n78\t                self.events.append(\"listening→thinking (max utterance)\")\n79\t                self._to_thinking()\n80\t            else:\n81\t                self.events.append(\"listening→idle (max, no speech)\")\n82\t                self._to_idle()\n83\t        elif self._speech < self.min_speech_frames and self._listen >= self.listen_timeout_frames:\n84\t            self.events.append(\"listening→idle (timeout, no speech)\")\n85\t            self._to_idle()\n86\t\n87\t    def _speaking(self, frame) -> None:\n88\t        # full-duplex barge-in with HYSTERESIS: only `barge_in_frames` CONSECUTIVE speech frames\n89\t        # count as a real interruption, so a single cough/click/TTS-echo blip can't self-abort the\n90\t        # answer. During the short confirm window the TTS keeps playing; on a silence frame the\n91\t        # counter resets. (A real mic+speaker still needs AEC or ducking — see the module note.)\n92\t        if self.vad.is_speech(frame):\n93\t            self._barge += 1\n94\t            self._barge_buf.append(frame)\n95\t            if self._barge >= self.barge_in_frames:\n96\t                self.events.append(\"barge-in: speaking→listening\")\n97\t                self._tts_iter = None\n98\t                self._to_listening(self._barge_buf, speech=self._barge)\n99\t            else:\n100\t                self._advance_tts()\n101\t        else:\n102\t            self._barge = 0\n103\t            self._barge_buf = []\n104\t            self._advance_tts()\n105\t\n106\t    def _advance_tts(self) -> None:\n107\t        try:\n108\t            self.sink.play(next(self._tts_iter))\n109\t        except StopIteration:\n110\t            self.events.append(\"speaking→idle\")\n111\t            self._to_idle()\n112\t\n113\t    # --- transitions -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n114\t    def _to_listening(self, captured: List, speech: int = 0) -> None:\n115\t        if self.state is State.IDLE:\n116\t            self.events.append(\"wake→listening\")\n117\t        self.state = State.LISTENING\n118\t        self._captured = list(captured)\n119\t        self._silence = 0\n120\t        self._speech = speech\n121\t        self._listen = len(captured)\n122\t\n123\t    def _to_thinking(self) -> None:\n124\t        self.events.append(\"listening→thinking\")\n125\t        self.state = State.THINKING\n126\t        # synchronous v1: transcribe → dispatch to the KERNEL → speak the response.\n127\t        self.transcript = self.asr.transcribe(self._captured)\n128\t        self.response = self.dispatch.send(self.transcript)\n129\t        self._to_speaking(self.response)\n130\t\n131\t    def _to_speaking(self, text: str) -> None:\n132\t        self.events.append(\"thinking→speaking\")\n133\t        self.state = State.SPEAKING\n134\t        self._tts_iter = iter(self.tts.synth(text))\n135\t        self._barge = 0\n136\t        self._barge_buf = []\n137\t\n138\t    def _to_idle(self) -> None:\n139\t        self.state = State.IDLE\n140\t        self._captured = []\n141\t        self._tts_iter = None\n142\t        self.wake.reset()\n143\t\n144\t    # --- driver: pump an audio source through the machine ------------------------------------\n145\t    def run(self, frames) -> None:\n146\t        \"\"\"Drive the pipeline from any iterable of input frames (a mic stream or a file). After a\n147\t        FINITE stream ends (file mode), drain any in-flight TTS so the whole answer is emitted\n148\t        (live/infinite streams play the TTS in real time, one chunk per input frame).\"\"\"\n149\t        for frame in frames:\n150\t            self.on_frame(frame)\n151\t        self.drain()\n152\t\n153\t    def drain(self) -> None:\n154\t        while self.state is State.SPEAKING and self._tts_iter is not None:\n155\t            self._advance_tts()\n156\t"}]},"uuid":"1c152804-aa79-4216-856a-474c10656ce1","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:25.083Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fb94dc00-73db-4fb6-903e-59905e762c18","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Two tiers:\n2\t\n3\t  * ZERO-dep, always-available: `EnergyVad` (RMS), `EnergyWake` (speech-onset gate),\n4\t    `FileAudioSource`/`FileSink` (stdlib `wave` + numpy), `SilenceTts` (fallback). These let the\n5\t    whole pipeline run on a WAV file with no model downloads.\n6\t  * OPTIONAL ML (lazy-imported, graceful if absent): `SileroVad`, `OpenWakeWord`, `WhisperAsr`\n7\t    (faster-whisper), `PiperTts`, `MicAudioSource`, `SpeakerSink`.\n8\t\n9\tThe pipeline (`pipeline.VoicePipeline`) is agnostic to which tier is wired.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom typing import Iterator, List\n13\t\n14\timport numpy as np\n15\t\n16\tfrom .components import FRAME_SAMPLES, SAMPLE_RATE\n17\t\n18\t\n19\t# ---- zero-dependency backends ---------------------------------------------------------------\n20\t\n21\tclass EnergyVad:\n22\t    \"\"\"RMS voice-activity detection over an int16 frame — no model. A reasonable default; Silero\n23\t    is the upgrade for noisy environments.\"\"\"\n24\t    def __init__(self, threshold: float = 500.0):\n25\t        self.threshold = threshold\n26\t\n27\t    def is_speech(self, frame) -> bool:\n28\t        x = np.asarray(frame, dtype=np.float32)\n29\t        if x.size == 0:\n30\t            return False\n31\t        return float(np.sqrt(np.mean(x * x))) >= self.threshold\n32\t\n33\t\n34\tclass EnergyWake:\n35\t    \"\"\"A trivial 'wake' that fires after `onset_frames` consecutive speech frames — a stand-in for\n36\t    the real custom-'SIGIL' openWakeWord model (which needs training data). Good for push-to-talk\n37\t    / always-listening use; swap in OpenWakeWord for hands-free wake.\"\"\"\n38\t    def __init__(self, vad: EnergyVad | None = None, onset_frames: int = 3):\n39\t        self.vad = vad or EnergyVad()\n40\t        self.onset_frames = onset_frames\n41\t        self._run = 0\n42\t\n43\t    def detect(self, frame) -> bool:\n44\t        if self.vad.is_speech(frame):\n45\t            self._run += 1\n46\t        else:\n47\t            self._run = 0\n48\t        if self._run >= self.onset_frames:\n49\t            self._run = 0\n50\t            return True\n51\t        return False\n52\t\n53\t    def reset(self) -> None:\n54\t        self._run = 0\n55\t\n56\t\n57\tdef _resample_i16(audio: np.ndarray, src: int, dst: int) -> np.ndarray:\n58\t    if src == dst or audio.size == 0:\n59\t        return audio\n60\t    n = int(round(len(audio) * dst / src))\n61\t    xp = np.linspace(0, 1, num=len(audio), endpoint=False)\n62\t    x = np.linspace(0, 1, num=n, endpoint=False)\n63\t    return np.interp(x, xp, audio.astype(np.float32)).astype(np.int16)\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tclass _FrameBuffer:\n67\t    \"\"\"Slice an arbitrary int16 stream into fixed-size frames, carrying the leftover across pushes\n68\t    (so no partial-frame audio is dropped between chunks); `flush` emits the final zero-padded frame.\"\"\"\n69\t    def __init__(self, frame_samples: int = FRAME_SAMPLES):\n70\t        self.n = frame_samples\n71\t        self._resid = np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int16)\n72\t\n73\t    def push(self, audio) -> Iterator[np.ndarray]:\n74\t        buf = np.concatenate([self._resid, np.asarray(audio, dtype=np.int16)])\n75\t        i = 0\n76\t        while i + self.n <= len(buf):\n77\t            yield buf[i:i + self.n]\n78\t            i += self.n\n79\t        self._resid = buf[i:].copy()\n80\t\n81\t    def flush(self) -> Iterator[np.ndarray]:\n82\t        if self._resid.size:\n83\t            frame = np.zeros(self.n, dtype=np.int16)\n84\t            frame[:self._resid.size] = self._resid\n85\t            self._resid = np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int16)\n86\t            yield frame\n87\t\n88\t\n89\tdef _decode_wav(raw: bytes, sampwidth: int) -> np.ndarray:\n90\t    if sampwidth == 2:\n91\t        return np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.int16)\n92\t    if sampwidth == 1:  # 8-bit unsigned PCM → int16\n93\t        return ((np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.uint8).astype(np.int32) - 128) * 256).astype(np.int16)\n94\t    raise ValueError(f\"unsupported WAV sample width {sampwidth * 8}-bit (need 8- or 16-bit PCM)\")\n95\t\n96\t\n97\tclass FileAudioSource:\n98\t    \"\"\"Yield 20-ms int16 mono frames from a WAV (stdlib `wave`), down-mixed + resampled to 16 kHz.\n99\t    Handles 8-/16-bit PCM, and emits the final (zero-padded) partial frame — no audio dropped.\"\"\"\n100\t    def __init__(self, path: str, frame_samples: int = FRAME_SAMPLES, rate: int = SAMPLE_RATE):\n101\t        self.path, self.frame_samples, self.rate = path, frame_samples, rate\n102\t\n103\t    def frames(self) -> Iterator[np.ndarray]:\n104\t        import wave\n105\t        with wave.open(self.path, \"rb\") as w:\n106\t            src_rate, ch, n, sw = w.getframerate(), w.getnchannels(), w.getnframes(), w.getsampwidth()\n107\t            raw = w.readframes(n)\n108\t        audio = _decode_wav(raw, sw)\n109\t        if ch > 1:\n110\t            audio = audio.reshape(-1, ch).mean(axis=1).astype(np.int16)\n111\t        audio = _resample_i16(audio, src_rate, self.rate)\n112\t        fb = _FrameBuffer(self.frame_samples)\n113\t        yield from fb.push(audio)\n114\t        yield from fb.flush()\n115\t\n116\t\n117\tclass FileSink:\n118\t    \"\"\"Collect played frames and write them to a WAV on close (16 kHz mono int16).\"\"\"\n119\t    def __init__(self, path: str, rate: int = SAMPLE_RATE):\n120\t        self.path, self.rate, self._buf = path, rate, []\n121\t\n122\t    def play(self, frame) -> None:\n123\t        arr = frame if isinstance(frame, np.ndarray) else np.zeros(FRAME_SAMPLES, dtype=np.int16)\n124\t        self._buf.append(np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.int16))\n125\t\n126\t    def close(self) -> None:\n127\t        import wave\n128\t        data = np.concatenate(self._buf) if self._buf else np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int16)\n129\t        with wave.open(self.path, \"wb\") as w:\n130\t            w.setnchannels(1); w.setsampwidth(2); w.setframerate(self.rate)\n131\t            w.writeframes(data.tobytes())\n132\t\n133\t\n134\tclass SilenceTts:\n135\t    \"\"\"Fallback TTS: emits ~`dur_per_char` of silence per character as 20-ms frames — so the\n136\t    full-duplex/barge-in pipeline runs (and its timing is realistic) without a real voice model.\"\"\"\n137\t    def __init__(self, ms_per_char: int = 60):\n138\t        self.ms_per_char = ms_per_char\n139\t\n140\t    def synth(self, text: str) -> Iterator[np.ndarray]:\n141\t        n_frames = max(1, (len(text) * self.ms_per_char) // 20)\n142\t        for _ in range(n_frames):\n143\t            yield np.zeros(FRAME_SAMPLES, dtype=np.int16)\n144\t\n145\t\n146\t# ---- optional ML backends (lazy imports) ----------------------------------------------------\n147\t\n148\tclass WhisperAsr:\n149\t    \"\"\"faster-whisper streaming ASR. `model` defaults to 'tiny' (fast; ~40 MB). Lazy-loaded.\"\"\"\n150\t    def __init__(self, model: str = \"tiny\", compute_type: str = \"int8\"):\n151\t        from faster_whisper import WhisperModel  # optional dep\n152\t        self._m = WhisperModel(model, device=\"cpu\", compute_type=compute_type)\n153\t\n154\t    def transcribe(self, frames: List) -> str:\n155\t        if not frames:\n156\t            return \"\"\n157\t        audio = np.concatenate([np.asarray(f, dtype=np.int16) for f in frames]).astype(np.float32) / 32768.0\n158\t        segments, _ = self._m.transcribe(audio, language=\"en\", vad_filter=False)\n159\t        return \" \".join(s.text.strip() for s in segments).strip()\n160\t\n161\t\n162\tclass SileroVad:\n163\t    def __init__(self, threshold: float = 0.5):\n164\t        import torch  # optional dep\n165\t        model, _ = torch.hub.load(\"snakers4/silero-vad\", \"silero_vad\", trust_repo=True)\n166\t        self._m, self._t, self._torch = model, threshold, torch\n167\t\n168\t    def is_speech(self, frame) -> bool:\n169\t        x = self._torch.from_numpy(np.asarray(frame, dtype=np.float32) / 32768.0)\n170\t        return float(self._m(x, SAMPLE_RATE).item()) >= self._t\n171\t\n172\t\n173\tclass OpenWakeWord:\n174\t    def __init__(self, model: str = \"hey_jarvis\", threshold: float = 0.5):\n175\t        from openwakeword.model import Model  # optional dep; custom 'SIGIL' model trains later\n176\t        self._m, self._t, self._name = Model(wakeword_models=[model]), threshold, model\n177\t\n178\t    def detect(self, frame) -> bool:\n179\t        scores = self._m.predict(np.asarray(frame, dtype=np.int16))\n180\t        return any(v >= self._t for v in scores.values())\n181\t\n182\t    def reset(self) -> None:\n183\t        try:\n184\t            self._m.reset()\n185\t        except Exception:\n186\t            pass\n187\t\n188\t\n189\tclass PiperTts:\n190\t    \"\"\"Piper local TTS. `voice` is a path to a .onnx voice model (downloaded separately).\"\"\"\n191\t    def __init__(self, voice: str):\n192\t        from piper.voice import PiperVoice  # optional dep\n193\t        self._v = PiperVoice.load(voice)\n194\t\n195\t    def synth(self, text: str) -> Iterator[np.ndarray]:\n196\t        fb = _FrameBuffer(FRAME_SAMPLES)\n197\t        for chunk in self._v.synthesize_stream_raw(text):\n198\t            yield from fb.push(np.frombuffer(chunk, dtype=np.int16))\n199\t        yield from fb.flush()\n200\t\n201\t\n202\tclass ElevenLabsTts:\n203\t    \"\"\"ElevenLabs cloud TTS (SIGIL owner's choice). Requests raw pcm_16000 so the pipeline can\n204\t    play/barge-in at the frame level. Needs ELEVENLABS_API_KEY (env or ~/.sigil/sigil.env). NOTE:\n205\t    a third-party service — the response TEXT leaves the machine; the local `PiperTts` is the\n206\t    sovereign alternative.\"\"\"\n207\t    def __init__(self, voice_id: str | None = None, model_id: str = \"eleven_flash_v2_5\",\n208\t                 api_key: str | None = None, timeout: int = 30):\n209\t        import os\n210\t        # the JARVIS voice_id (found via find_voices/`--find-voice jarvis`) is persisted as\n211\t        # SIGIL_TTS_VOICE_ID; fall back to the stock 'Rachel' only if nothing is configured.\n212\t        self.voice_id = voice_id or os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_TTS_VOICE_ID\") or \"21m00Tcm4TlvDq8ikWAM\"\n213\t        self.model_id, self.timeout = model_id, timeout\n214\t        self.api_key = api_key or os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_ELEVENLABS_API_KEY\") or os.environ.get(\"ELEVENLABS_API_KEY\")\n215\t\n216\t    def synth(self, text: str) -> Iterator[np.ndarray]:\n217\t        import json\n218\t        import urllib.request\n219\t        if not self.api_key:\n220\t            raise RuntimeError(\"ElevenLabsTts needs ELEVENLABS_API_KEY (env or ~/.sigil/sigil.env)\")\n221\t        url = (f\"https://api.elevenlabs.io/v1/text-to-speech/{self.voice_id}/stream\"\n222\t               f\"?output_format=pcm_16000\")\n223\t        body = json.dumps({\"text\": text, \"model_id\": self.model_id}).encode(\"utf-8\")\n224\t        req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=body, method=\"POST\",\n225\t                                     headers={\"xi-api-key\": self.api_key, \"content-type\": \"application/json\"})\n226\t        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:\n227\t            pcm = resp.read()\n228\t        fb = _FrameBuffer(FRAME_SAMPLES)\n229\t        yield from fb.push(np.frombuffer(pcm, dtype=np.int16))\n230\t        yield from fb.flush()\n231\t\n232\t\n233\tclass ElevenLabsAsr:\n234\t    \"\"\"ElevenLabs 'Scribe' cloud STT (SIGIL owner's choice). Sends the captured utterance as a WAV\n235\t    to /v1/speech-to-text. Needs ELEVENLABS_API_KEY. NOTE: a third-party service — the AUDIO leaves\n236\t    the machine; the local `WhisperAsr` is the sovereign alternative.\"\"\"\n237\t    def __init__(self, model_id: str = \"scribe_v1\", api_key: str | None = None, timeout: int = 30):\n238\t        import os\n239\t        self.model_id, self.timeout = model_id, timeout\n240\t        self.api_key = api_key or os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_ELEVENLABS_API_KEY\") or os.environ.get(\"ELEVENLABS_API_KEY\")\n241\t\n242\t    def transcribe(self, frames: List) -> str:\n243\t        import io\n244\t        import json\n245\t        import urllib.request\n246\t        import uuid\n247\t        import wave\n248\t        if not self.api_key:\n249\t            raise RuntimeError(\"ElevenLabsAsr needs ELEVENLABS_API_KEY (env or ~/.sigil/sigil.env)\")\n250\t        if not frames:\n251\t            return \"\"\n252\t        # assemble the frames into an in-memory 16 kHz mono WAV\n253\t        audio = np.concatenate([np.asarray(f, dtype=np.int16) for f in frames])\n254\t        buf = io.BytesIO()\n255\t        with wave.open(buf, \"wb\") as w:\n256\t            w.setnchannels(1); w.setsampwidth(2); w.setframerate(SAMPLE_RATE); w.writeframes(audio.tobytes())\n257\t        wav_bytes = buf.getvalue()\n258\t        # minimal multipart/form-data (model_id + file)\n259\t        boundary = f\"----sigil{uuid.uuid4().hex}\"\n260\t        parts = [\n261\t            f\"--{boundary}\\r\\nContent-Disposition: form-data; name=\\\"model_id\\\"\\r\\n\\r\\n{self.model_id}\\r\\n\".encode(),\n262\t            (f\"--{boundary}\\r\\nContent-Disposition: form-data; name=\\\"file\\\"; filename=\\\"a.wav\\\"\\r\\n\"\n263\t             f\"Content-Type: audio/wav\\r\\n\\r\\n\").encode(),\n264\t            wav_bytes, b\"\\r\\n\", f\"--{boundary}--\\r\\n\".encode(),\n265\t        ]\n266\t        req = urllib.request.Request(\n267\t            \"https://api.elevenlabs.io/v1/speech-to-text\", data=b\"\".join(parts), method=\"POST\",\n268\t            headers={\"xi-api-key\": self.api_key, \"content-type\": f\"multipart/form-data; boundary={boundary}\"})\n269\t        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:\n270\t            payload = json.loads(resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n271\t        return str(payload.get(\"text\", \"\")).strip()\n272\t\n273\t\n274\tdef find_voices(query: str = \"jarvis\", api_key: str | None = None, timeout: int = 30) -> List[dict]:\n275\t    \"\"\"Search the ElevenLabs SHARED voice library for `query` (e.g. 'jarvis' → the Iron-Man-style\n276\t    British butler voice). Returns [{name, voice_id, accent, category, description}] so the owner\n277\t    can pick + pin one via `set_voice`.\"\"\"\n278\t    import json\n279\t    import os\n280\t    import urllib.parse\n281\t    import urllib.request\n282\t    key = api_key or os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_ELEVENLABS_API_KEY\") or os.environ.get(\"ELEVENLABS_API_KEY\")\n283\t    if not key:\n284\t        raise RuntimeError(\"find_voices needs ELEVENLABS_API_KEY (env or ~/.sigil/sigil.env)\")\n285\t    url = \"https://api.elevenlabs.io/v1/shared-voices?\" + urllib.parse.urlencode({\"search\": query, \"page_size\": 25})\n286\t    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={\"xi-api-key\": key})\n287\t    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:\n288\t        payload = json.loads(resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n289\t    out = []\n290\t    for v in payload.get(\"voices\", []):\n291\t        langs = v.get(\"verified_languages\") or []\n292\t        out.append({\n293\t            \"name\": v.get(\"name\"),\n294\t            \"voice_id\": v.get(\"voice_id\"),\n295\t            \"accent\": v.get(\"accent\") or (langs[0].get(\"accent\", \"\") if langs else \"\"),\n296\t            \"category\": v.get(\"category\"),\n297\t            \"description\": (v.get(\"description\") or \"\")[:90],\n298\t        })\n299\t    return out\n300\t\n301\t\n302\tdef set_voice(voice_id: str) -> None:\n303\t    \"\"\"Persist the chosen TTS voice_id as SIGIL_TTS_VOICE_ID in ~/.sigil/sigil.env (loaded by\n304\t    config at import), so ElevenLabsTts uses it by default everywhere.\"\"\"\n305\t    from ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n306\t    env_path = SIGIL_HOME / \"sigil.env\"\n307\t    lines = []\n308\t    if env_path.exists():\n309\t        lines = [ln for ln in env_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()\n310\t                 if not ln.strip().startswith(\"SIGIL_TTS_VOICE_ID=\")]\n311\t    lines.append(f\"SIGIL_TTS_VOICE_ID={voice_id}\")\n312\t    env_path.write_text(\"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n313\t\n314\t\n315\tclass MicAudioSource:\n316\t    def __init__(self, frame_samples: int = FRAME_SAMPLES, rate: int = SAMPLE_RATE):\n317\t        self.frame_samples, self.rate = frame_samples, rate\n318\t\n319\t    def frames(self) -> Iterator[np.ndarray]:\n320\t        import sounddevice as sd  # optional dep\n321\t        with sd.InputStream(samplerate=self.rate, channels=1, dtype=\"int16\", blocksize=self.frame_samples) as stream:\n322\t            while True:\n323\t                data, _ = stream.read(self.frame_samples)\n324\t                yield data[:, 0].copy()\n325\t\n326\t\n327\tclass SpeakerSink:\n328\t    def __init__(self, rate: int = self._stream.start()\n332\t\n333\t    def play(self, frame) -> None:\n334\t        self._stream.write(np.asarray(frame, dtype=np.int16))\n335\t\n336\t    def close(self) -> None:\n337\t        try:\n338\t            self._stream.stop(); self._stream.close()\n339\t        except Exception:\n340\t            pass\n341\t"}]},"uuid":"d88b312a-9a32-4b9c-93fa-972cda32dbfa","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:25.604Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8c0d4017-e566-46cd-a50a-8d78c94535a4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"6a40fddb-f0f2-4952-aa5a-2430a89836aa","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAA3btnmM6Tn5guqstqQ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01WhLBwsi2eSPc1tBK1qPxxX","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/perception/capture.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":10770,"cache_read_input_tokens":14778,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":10770,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAA3aNkueDJQj5Uic5E3","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"2bc5c324-c6ce-46e1-b69e-45e5c9d9742f","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:31.425Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"2bc5c324-c6ce-46e1-b69e-45e5c9d9742f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WhLBwsi2eSPc1tBK1qPxxX","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Capture backends (SIGIL §8). A `Frame` is the immutable record of one capture: the image bytes'\n2\tsha256 (integrity), the OCR/accessibility TEXT (the GROUND TRUTH a query is answered from), and a\n3\tpath to the image. Real backends shell out to whatever is installed (screenshot tool + tesseract,\n4\tv4l2/ffmpeg); every backend returns `None` on any failure — a missing camera is no capture, never a\n5\tfabricated one (same posture as SCHOLAR.read_source). `StaticFrame` is the deterministic test double.\n6\t\n7\tHONEST GAPS: headless hosts often lack a screenshot tool / tesseract / a camera, so the real\n8\tbackends degrade to `None` or an image-only Frame (text=\"\"). The richest real screen source is the\n9\tOS accessibility tree (C4); it is not available on a headless box and is left as a documented seam.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport shutil\n13\timport subprocess\n14\timport tempfile\n15\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n16\tfrom pathlib import Path\n17\tfrom typing import Optional\n18\t\n19\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n20\t\n21\t_SCREENSHOT_TOOLS = (\n22\t    (\"scrot\", [\"scrot\", \"-o\"]),\n23\t    (\"gnome-screenshot\", [\"gnome-screenshot\", \"-f\"]),\n24\t    (\"spectacle\", [\"spectacle\", \"-b\", \"-n\", \"-o\"]),\n25\t    (\"import\", [\"import\", \"-window\", \"root\"]),   # ImageMagick\n26\t)\n27\t\n28\t\n29\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n30\tclass Frame:\n31\t    kind: str                       # \"screen\" | \"camera\"\n32\t    sha256: str                     # integrity of the captured image bytes\n33\t    text: str                       # OCR / accessibility text — the GROUND TRUTH; \"\" if none\n34\t    image_path: Optional[str] = None\n35\t    width: int = 0\n36\t    height: int = 0\n37\t\n38\t    @classmethod\n39\t    def from_image(cls, kind: str, image_path: str, *, text: str = \"\") -> \"Frame\":\n40\t        data = Path(image_path).read_bytes()\n41\t        return cls(kind=kind, sha256=sha256_hex(data), text=text, image_path=image_path)\n42\t\n43\t\n44\tdef _ocr(image_path: str) -> str:\n45\t    \"\"\"Extract text via tesseract if present; '' otherwise (no OCR is not a failure of capture).\"\"\"\n46\t    if not shutil.which(\"tesseract\"):\n47\t        return \"\"\n48\t    try:\n49\t        proc = subprocess.run([\"tesseract\", image_path, \"stdout\"],\n50\t                              capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)\n51\t        return (proc.stdout or \"\").strip()\n52\t    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n53\t        return \"\"\n54\t\n55\t\n56\tdef grab_screen() -> Optional[Frame]:\n57\t    \"\"\"Screenshot the current display + OCR it. Returns None if no screenshot tool is available\n58\t    or the grab fails. The accessibility-tree path (richer, C4) is a documented seam, not wired\n59\t    on headless hosts.\"\"\"\n60\t    out = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".png\", prefix=\"sigil-screen-\")\n61\t    for name, argv in _SCREENSHOT_TOOLS:\n62\t        if not shutil.which(name):\n63\t            continue\n64\t        try:\n65\t            proc = subprocess.run([*argv, out], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20)\n66\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n67\t            continue\n68\t        if proc.returncode == 0 and Path(out).exists() and Path(out).stat().st_size > 0:\n69\t            return Frame.from_image(\"screen\", out, text=_ocr(out))\n70\t    return None\n71\t\n72\t\n73\tdef grab_camera(device: str = \"/dev/video0\") -> Optional[Frame]:\n74\t    \"\"\"Grab a single frame from a v4l2 camera via ffmpeg. Returns None if ffmpeg or the device is\n75\t    absent, or the grab fails. Camera frames rarely carry text, so OCR is best-effort.\"\"\"\n76\t    if not shutil.which(\"ffmpeg\") or not Path(device).exists():\n77\t        return None\n78\t    out = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".png\", prefix=\"sigil-cam-\")\n79\t    try:\n80\t        proc = subprocess.run(\n81\t            [\"ffmpeg\", \"-y\", \"-f\", \"v4l2\", \"-i\", device, \"-frames:v\", \"1\", out],\n82\t            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20)\n83\t    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n84\t        return None\n85\t    if proc.returncode == 0 and Path(out).exists() and Path(out).stat().st_size > 0:\n86\t        return Frame.from_image(\"camera\", out, text=_ocr(out))\n87\t    return None\n88\t\n89\t\n90\tdef StaticFrame(*, kind: str = \"screen\", text: str = \"\", tag: str = \"\") -> Frame:\n91\t    \"\"\"Deterministic test/double frame — no filesystem, sha256 derived from (tag or text) so\n92\t    identical content hashes identically (drives ambient change-detection).\"\"\"\n93\t    return Frame(kind=kind, sha256=sha256_hex((tag or text).encode()), text=text, image_path=None)\n94\t"}]},"uuid":"b4a4e07b-f8de-469a-9805-e9358c7e9229","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:31.462Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2bc5c324-c6ce-46e1-b69e-45e5c9d9742f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"b4a4e07b-f8de-469a-9805-e9358c7e9229","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAA3btnmM6Tn5guqstqQ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01HkzKTpi16uQFbWRkHq2Mbs","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/perception/vision.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":10770,"cache_read_input_tokens":14778,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":10770,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAA3aNkueDJQj5Uic5E3","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"e64b5dbc-c6ac-4dfa-936a-3c9e1a192d63","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:31.929Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"e64b5dbc-c6ac-4dfa-936a-3c9e1a192d63","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HkzKTpi16uQFbWRkHq2Mbs","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The VLM seam (SIGIL §8 / C4-C6). A `VisionModel` turns a captured Frame + a question into a\n2\tfree-text visual reading. That reading is ADVISORY — the perception layer serves the frame's\n3\tcaptured OCR TEXT as the authoritative answer and labels the model's reading as unverified (see\n4\t`perceive.compose_perception`). Cascade doctrine: a cheap LOCAL model runs freely (on-box, A0); the\n5\tFRONTIER model is a data-egress hop and is gated (see `perception.egress`).\n6\t\n7\tProviders:\n8\t  • `MoondreamVision` — a local moondream-class VLM via Ollama. Fully offline, zero egress → A0.\n9\t    The default reader. Returns '' on any failure (honest empty, never a fabricated reading).\n10\t  • `ClaudeVision` — the frontier Anthropic vision API. `describe()` performs a REAL image UPLOAD\n11\t    (private screen bytes leave the box), so it MUST only be invoked through `perception.egress`,\n12\t    which classifies the upload A2 via the WARDEN oracle and requires a verified owner approval.\n13\t    Never call `ClaudeVision.describe` on an auto path.\"\"\"\n14\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n15\t\n16\timport base64\n17\timport json\n18\timport os\n19\timport urllib.error\n20\timport urllib.request\n21\tfrom pathlib import Path\n22\tfrom typing import Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n23\t\n24\tfrom .capture import Frame\n25\t\n26\t\n27\t@runtime_checkable\n28\tclass VisionModel(Protocol):\n29\t    # `egresses` = does describe() send bytes off the box? On-box models (moondream) are False and may\n30\t    # run on the auto A0 path; frontier models are True and may ONLY be invoked through the egress gate\n31\t    # (`Perceptor.frontier`). `perceive`/`ambient_watch` STRUCTURALLY refuse an egressing model.\n32\t    egresses: bool\n33\t    def describe(self, frame: Frame, question: str) -> str: ...   # advisory free-text reading\n34\t\n35\t\n36\tdef _b64_image(frame: Frame) -> Optional[str]:\n37\t    if not frame.image_path:\n38\t        return None\n39\t    try:\n40\t        return base64.b64encode(Path(frame.image_path).read_bytes()).decode(\"ascii\")\n41\t    except OSError:\n42\t        return None\n43\t\n44\t\n45\tclass MoondreamVision:\n46\t    \"\"\"Local VLM (Ollama, on-box, A0 — nothing leaves the machine). POSTs the frame image + question\n47\t    to `{host}/api/generate` with `images:[b64]`. Mirrors `consolidate.extract.LocalProvider`.\"\"\"\n48\t    egresses = False   # on-box — safe on the auto perceive/ambient path\n49\t\n50\t    def __init__(self, model: str = \"moondream\", host: Optional[str] = None, timeout: int = 60):\n51\t        self.model = model\n52\t        self.host = host or os.environ.get(\"OLLAMA_HOST\") or \"http://127.0.0.1:11434\"\n53\t        self.timeout = timeout\n54\t\n55\t    def describe(self, frame: Frame, question: str) -> str:\n56\t        b64 = _b64_image(frame)\n57\t        if b64 is None:\n58\t            return \"\"\n59\t        body = json.dumps({\"model\": self.model, \"prompt\": (question or \"Describe what is visible.\"),\n60\t                           \"images\": [b64], \"stream\": False}).encode(\"utf-8\")\n61\t        req = urllib.request.Request(f\"{self.host.rstrip('/')}/api/generate\", data=body, method=\"POST\",\n62\t                                     headers={\"content-type\": \"application/json\"})\n63\t        try:\n64\t            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:\n65\t                payload = json.loads(resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n66\t        except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError, ValueError):\n67\t            return \"\"   # no reading (honest empty), never a fabricated one\n68\t        return str(payload.get(\"response\", \"\")).strip()[:2000]\n69\t\n70\t\n71\tclass ClaudeVision:\n72\t    \"\"\"FRONTIER Anthropic vision API — `describe()` UPLOADS the image (data egress). Gate it via\n73\t    `perception.egress`; do not call directly on an auto path. Mirrors `extract.ApiProvider`.\"\"\"\n74\t    egresses = True    # sends bytes off-box — perceive/ambient refuse it; only frontier() may use it\n75\t\n76\t    def __init__(self, model: str = \"claude-sonnet-5\", api_key: Optional[str] = None,\n77\t                 timeout: int = 60, max_tokens: int = 512):\n78\t        self.model = model\n79\t        self.api_key = api_key or os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\") or os.environ.get(\"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\")\n80\t        self.timeout = timeout\n81\t        self.max_tokens = max_tokens\n82\t\n83\t    def describe(self, frame: Frame, question: str) -> str:\n84\t        b64 = _b64_image(frame)\n85\t        if b64 is None or not self.api_key:\n86\t            return \"\"\n87\t        media = \"image/png\" if (frame.image_path or \"\").lower().endswith(\".png\") else \"image/jpeg\"\n88\t        body = json.dumps({\n89\t            \"model\": self.model, \"max_tokens\": self.max_tokens,\n90\t            \"messages\": [{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": [\n91\t                {\"type\": \"image\", \"source\": {\"type\": \"base64\", \"media_type\": media, \"data\": b64}},\n92\t                {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": (question or \"Describe what is visible.\")},\n93\t            ]}],\n94\t        }).encode(\"utf-8\")\n95\t        req = urllib.request.Request(\n96\t            \"https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages\", data=body, method=\"POST\",\n97\t            headers={\"content-type\": \"application/json\", \"x-api-key\": self.api_key,\n98\t                     \"anthropic-version\": \"2023-06-01\"})\n99\t        try:\n100\t            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:\n101\t                payload = json.loads(resp.read().decode(\"utf-8\"))\n102\t        except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError, ValueError):\n103\t            return \"\"\n104\t        return \"\".join(b.get(\"text\", \"\") for b in payload.get(\"content\", [])\n105\t                       if isinstance(b, dict)).strip()[:2000]\n106\t"}]},"uuid":"8b486a6e-f600-4cd7-92ed-568a2471cc25","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:31.944Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e64b5dbc-c6ac-4dfa-936a-3c9e1a192d63","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"8b486a6e-f600-4cd7-92ed-568a2471cc25","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAA3btnmM6Tn5guqstqQ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01DK4VCysh4ATgDrgbovv8Qm","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/platform/base.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":10770,"cache_read_input_tokens":14778,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":10770,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAA3aNkueDJQj5Uic5E3","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"0e53ed1d-aa13-44a3-bc24-748ff89ba28e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:32.435Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"0e53ed1d-aa13-44a3-bc24-748ff89ba28e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DK4VCysh4ATgDrgbovv8Qm","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The host-backend contract (Phase 7, WS-D). A `HostBackend` captures screen/camera into the same\n2\t`Frame` the perception stack already consumes, and advertises what the host can do. The capability\n3\tdescriptor is what the mesh routes on (see `sigil.mesh`): which host has a camera, a GPU for a local\n4\tVLM, and is always-on — so vision/compute go to the best-capable ONLINE host while authority stays\n5\tin the one signed spine.\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n9\tfrom typing import Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n10\t\n11\tfrom ..perception.capture import Frame\n12\t\n13\t\n14\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n15\tclass CapabilityDescriptor:\n16\t    host_id: str\n17\t    os: str\n18\t    has_screen: bool\n19\t    has_camera: bool\n20\t    has_gpu_vlm: bool          # a local moondream-class VLM is reachable (Ollama up)\n21\t    always_on: bool\n22\t\n23\t    def to_dict(self) -> dict:\n24\t        return {\"host_id\": self.host_id, \"os\": self.os, \"has_screen\": self.has_screen,\n25\t                \"has_camera\": self.has_camera, \"has_gpu_vlm\": self.has_gpu_vlm, \"always_on\": self.always_on}\n26\t\n27\t\n28\t@runtime_checkable\n29\tclass HostBackend(Protocol):\n30\t    def capture_screen(self) -> Optional[Frame]: ...\n31\t    def capture_camera(self) -> Optional[Frame]: ...\n32\t    def capabilities(self) -> CapabilityDescriptor: ...\n33\t\n34\t\n35\t# --- shared, OS-agnostic probes ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n36\tdef host_id() -> str:\n37\t    \"\"\"A stable per-host id (persisted once under SIGIL_HOME). Identifies this host in the mesh.\"\"\"\n38\t    import uuid\n39\t\n40\t    from ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n41\t    f = SIGIL_HOME / \"host_id\"\n42\t    try:\n43\t        v = f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip()\n44\t        if v:\n45\t            return v\n46\t    except OSError:\n47\t        pass\n48\t    SIGIL_HOME.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n49\t    hid = uuid.uuid4().hex[:16]\n50\t    try:\n51\t        f.write_text(hid, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n52\t    except OSError:\n53\t        pass\n54\t    return hid\n55\t\n56\t\n57\tdef probe_gpu_vlm() -> bool:\n58\t    \"\"\"True iff a local moondream-class VLM endpoint (Ollama) is reachable — cheap TCP probe.\"\"\"\n59\t    import os\n60\t    import socket\n61\t    from urllib.parse import urlparse\n62\t    u = urlparse(os.environ.get(\"OLLAMA_HOST\", \"http://127.0.0.1:11434\"))\n63\t    try:\n64\t        with socket.create_connection((u.hostname or \"127.0.0.1\", u.port or 11434), timeout=0.5):\n65\t            return True\n66\t    except OSError:\n67\t        return False\n68\t\n69\t\n70\tdef probe_always_on() -> bool:\n71\t    import os\n72\t    return os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_ALWAYS_ON\", \"\").lower() in (\"1\", \"true\", \"yes\")\n73\t"}]},"uuid":"4cc8a5f3-2030-464c-a568-cc5f63abb39f","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:32.480Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0e53ed1d-aa13-44a3-bc24-748ff89ba28e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"558b43e3-ba16-47d5-b93a-0bc060c9d721","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018izrtYDR3NTxbGWRRxrkgw","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Linux backend (Phase 7, WS-D) — wraps the existing perception capture verbatim (behaviour-\n2\tpreserving; the whole stack above is unchanged).\"\"\"\n3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n4\t\n5\timport shutil\n6\tfrom pathlib import Path\n7\tfrom typing import Optional\n8\t\n9\tfrom ..perception.capture import Frame, grab_camera, grab_screen\n10\tfrom .base import CapabilityDescriptor, host_id, probe_always_on, probe_gpu_vlm\n11\t\n12\t_SCREEN_TOOLS = (\"scrot\", \"gnome-screenshot\", \"spectacle\", \"import\")\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tclass LinuxBackend:\n16\t    def capture_screen(self) -> Optional[Frame]:\n17\t        return grab_screen()\n18\t\n19\t    def capture_camera(self) -> Optional[Frame]:\n20\t        return grab_camera()\n21\t\n22\t    def capabilities(self) -> CapabilityDescriptor:\n23\t        return CapabilityDescriptor(\n24\t            host_id=host_id(), os=\"linux\",\n25\t            has_screen=any(shutil.which(t) for t in _SCREEN_TOOLS),\n26\t            has_camera=Path(\"/dev/video0\").exists(),\n27\t            has_gpu_vlm=probe_gpu_vlm(), always_on=probe_always_on())\n28\t"}]},"uuid":"dcac33b4-ed00-4b43-8bf8-4de8100b6121","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:32.785Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"558b43e3-ba16-47d5-b93a-0bc060c9d721","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"f2bde1a8-e54a-4b50-b4e3-af941d6c14fa","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAA44s1rU7UtTAM1iWTT","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01DMjazp6Ws9rah9k5gvKdtq","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/kernel_classify.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6303,"cache_read_input_tokens":25548,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6303,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAA43kJJfHrNCYxYXb7o","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"ece3e0b0-5230-4763-8a44-030c119eb737","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:38.023Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"ece3e0b0-5230-4763-8a44-030c119eb737","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DMjazp6Ws9rah9k5gvKdtq","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"KernelClassifier (Phase 7, P0.1) — the Python↔Rust WARDEN bridge. The authoritative danger\n2\tclassifier is Rust `kernel/src/tiers.rs::classify` (token-based, danger-first, fail-closed to A3),\n3\tcomposed with the raise-only registry pins in `Warden::decide`. Python's `Governor` otherwise\n4\tTRUSTS a Proposal's declared tier; this bridge lets the mesh DERIVE a tool's tier from that\n5\tfail-closed oracle instead of self-declaring it — so an Operator step or a Vision egress hop is\n6\ttiered by the same code that enforces at the kernel.\n7\t\n8\tConsulting the kernel is a pure A0 observation (`sigil-kernel classify` writes NO action-log\n9\trecord). Any failure — missing binary, timeout, non-zero exit, unparseable output, or an unknown\n10\ttier string — resolves to **A3** (fail-closed to the most-gated tier), mirroring the Rust\n11\tclassifier's own posture: never let a bridge error silently downgrade a dangerous tool.\"\"\"\n12\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n13\t\n14\timport json\n15\timport subprocess\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..voice.dispatch import _default_bin  # reuse the exact kernel-binary resolution\n18\tfrom .base import Tier\n19\t\n20\t_TIER = {\"A0\": Tier.A0, \"A1\": Tier.A1, \"A2\": Tier.A2, \"A3\": Tier.A3}\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tclass KernelClassifier:\n24\t    def __init__(self, kernel_bin: str | None = None, timeout: int = 15):\n25\t        self.kernel_bin = kernel_bin or _default_bin()\n26\t        self.timeout = timeout\n27\t\n28\t    def classify(self, tool: str) -> Tier:\n29\t        \"\"\"Return the WARDEN tier for `tool`. Fail-closed to A3 on ANY error/ambiguity.\"\"\"\n30\t        if not tool or not tool.strip():\n31\t            return Tier.A3\n32\t        try:\n33\t            proc = subprocess.run([self.kernel_bin, \"classify\", tool, \"--json\"],\n34\t                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=self.timeout)\n35\t        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n36\t            return Tier.A3\n37\t        if proc.returncode != 0:\n38\t            return Tier.A3\n39\t        try:\n40\t            data = json.loads(proc.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])\n41\t            return _TIER.get(str(data.get(\"tier\")), Tier.A3)\n42\t        except (ValueError, IndexError, KeyError, TypeError):\n43\t            return Tier.A3\n44\t"}]},"uuid":"03fbc201-fc8a-4241-aec6-2b11d3d46b6b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:38.032Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ece3e0b0-5230-4763-8a44-030c119eb737","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"0d565468-6095-4c51-8916-7c84588ce7f4","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAA44s1rU7UtTAM1iWTT","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01N7Mafh72pG4dvn1jHENkzb","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/mesh/registry.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6303,"cache_read_input_tokens":25548,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6303,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAA43kJJfHrNCYxYXb7o","attributionAgent":"Plan","type":"assistant","uuid":"d07775b1-41b8-4b98-9a40-4930ab529f0e","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:38.963Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"0d565468-6095-4c51-8916-7c84588ce7f4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SXvLmrAjhoP7dzZheNSGFY","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//! WARDEN autonomy tiers (SIGIL §5). Every tool invocation is classified A0–A3; the gate\n2\t//! decision follows from the tier. FAIL-CLOSED and TOKEN-BASED: the tool name is split into\n3\t//! whole tokens (on `.`/`_`/`-`/`/`/space) and matched against whole-token sets — never a raw\n4\t//! substring, so \"overwrite\" is not \"write\" and \"forget\" is not \"get\". Danger is checked FIRST,\n5\t//! and A0 (auto, un-queued) is reachable ONLY via a positive safe-verb allowlist; anything not\n6\t//! positively classified — including any unknown or dangerous-target tool — is A3.\n7\t\n8\tuse std::fmt;\n9\t\n10\tuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n11\t\n12\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n13\tpub enum Tier {\n14\t    /// Observe / answer — memory queries, screen read, voice reply. Auto.\n15\t    A0,\n16\t    /// Reversible internal act — write a report/brief, draft-branch, raise an alert. Auto + logged.\n17\t    A1,\n18\t    /// External-visible / semi-reversible — send email, calendar write, purchase < cap. Queued.\n19\t    A2,\n20\t    /// Destructive / financial / security — push protected branch, deploy, delete, spend > cap. Explicit.\n21\t    A3,\n22\t}\n23\t\n24\timpl Tier {\n25\t    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {\n26\t        match self {\n27\t            Tier::A0 => \"A0\",\n28\t            Tier::A1 => \"A1\",\n29\t            Tier::A2 => \"A2\",\n30\t            Tier::A3 => \"A3\",\n31\t        }\n32\t    }\n33\t}\n34\t\n35\timpl fmt::Display for Tier {\n36\t    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n37\t        f.write_str(self.as_str())\n38\t    }\n39\t}\n40\t\n41\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n42\tpub enum Decision {\n43\t    Auto,\n44\t    Queued,\n45\t    ExplicitRequired,\n46\t}\n47\t\n48\timpl Decision {\n49\t    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str {\n50\t        match self {\n51\t            Decision::Auto => \"auto\",\n52\t            Decision::Queued => \"queued\",\n53\t            Decision::ExplicitRequired => \"explicit-required\",\n54\t        }\n55\t    }\n56\t    pub fn may_run(&self) -> bool {\n57\t        matches!(self, Decision::Auto)\n58\t    }\n59\t}\n60\t\n61\t/// A3 — destructive verbs, financial ops, crypto/restore ops, and DANGEROUS TARGETS\n62\t/// (secret/identity/network/prod material is A3 regardless of the verb: `secrets.read`,\n63\t/// `iam.policy.write`, `budget.get`, `vault.read`).\n64\tconst A3_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n65\t    // destructive verbs\n66\t    \"push\", \"deploy\", \"delete\", \"destroy\", \"drop\", \"remove\", \"purge\", \"wipe\", \"truncate\",\n67\t    \"overwrite\", \"erase\", \"format\", \"kill\", \"shutdown\", \"reboot\", \"reset\", \"disable\", \"enable\",\n68\t    \"override\", \"force\", \"sudo\", \"exec\", \"eval\", \"chmod\", \"chown\", \"patch\", \"install\", \"uninstall\",\n69\t    // crypto + restore/rollback (a restore silently reverts current state)\n70\t    \"encrypt\", \"decrypt\", \"restore\", \"revert\", \"rollback\", \"recover\",\n71\t    // financial\n72\t    \"spend\", \"purchase\", \"pay\", \"payment\", \"transaction\", \"transfer\", \"refund\", \"invoice\",\n73\t    \"allocate\", \"budget\", \"release\", \"sign\",\n74\t    // security / identity / infra / secret-store TARGETS (dangerous no matter the verb)\n75\t    \"secret\", \"secrets\", \"credential\", \"credentials\", \"token\", \"tokens\", \"key\", \"keys\", \"iam\",\n76\t    \"policy\", \"firewall\", \"acl\", \"role\", \"grant\", \"revoke\", \"rotate\", \"escalate\", \"infra\",\n77\t    \"prod\", \"production\", \"env\", \"master\", \"root\", \"admin\",\n78\t    \"vault\", \"keyring\", \"keychain\", \"keystore\", \"hsm\",\n79\t];\n80\t\n81\t/// A2 — external-visible / semi-reversible: communication, publishing, and bulk DATA EGRESS\n82\t/// (export/dump of a private store must be human-seen, never auto).\n83\tconst A2_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n84\t    \"send\", \"email\", \"smtp\", \"publish\", \"post\", \"message\", \"outbound\", \"webhook\", \"sms\", \"notify\",\n85\t    \"share\", \"upload\", \"invite\", \"calendar\", \"tweet\", \"dm\", \"export\", \"dump\", \"download\", \"sync\",\n86\t];\n87\t\n88\t/// A1 — reversible internal writes (drafts, notes, memory writes, non-push commits). NOTE:\n89\t/// \"snapshot\" is intentionally NOT here — a snapshot RESTORE is destructive (caught by A3\n90\t/// \"restore\"), and snapshot-create alone falls to fail-closed A3 rather than risk auto-run.\n91\tconst A1_TOKENS: &[&str] = &[\n92\t    \"write\", \"note\", \"brief\", \"report\", \"draft\", \"alert\", \"consolidate\", \"commit\", \"branch\",\n93\t    \"annotate\", \"tag\", \"label\",\n94\t];\n95\t\n96\t/// A0 — POSITIVE allowlist of known-safe observe/answer VERBS only (NO target nouns — a target\n97\t/// noun here would let e.g. `memory.encrypt` auto-run). A tool reaches A0 only by a read-verb\n98\t/// here, or an exact name in `A0_TOOLS`; absence of danger alone is NOT sufficient → fail-closed A3.\n99\tconst A0_VERBS: &[&str] = &[\n100\t    \"read\", \"search\", \"query\", \"get\", \"list\", \"status\", \"recall\", \"observe\", \"answer\",\n101\t    \"view\", \"show\", \"find\", \"frame\", \"peek\", \"describe\", \"inspect\", \"lookup\",\n102\t];\n103\t\n104\t/// A0 — exact-name allowlist for the known read-only memory tools whose names are not verb-clean\n105\t/// (`graph.entity`, `episodic.range`, `threads.open`, `commitments.due`, `contradictions.pending`,\n106\t/// `ingest.status`). Matched on the whole lowercased tool name.\n107\tconst A0_TOOLS: &[&str] = &[\n108\t    \"memory.search\", \"graph.query\", \"graph.entity\", \"episodic.range\", \"ingest.status\",\n109\t    \"threads.open\", \"commitments.due\", \"contradictions.pending\",\n110\t];\n111\t\n112\t/// Split a tool name into whole tokens on `.`/`_`/`-`/`/` and whitespace, lowercased.\n113\tfn tokens(tool: &str) -> Vec<String> {\n114\t    tool.split(|c: char| c == '.' || c == '_' || c == '-' || c == '/' || c.is_whitespace())\n115\t        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())\n116\t        .map(|s| s.to_ascii_lowercase())\n117\t        .collect()\n118\t}\n119\t\n120\t/// Classify a tool name into a tier. Danger FIRST, then A2, A1; A0 only via a positive safe-verb\n121\t/// or an exact safe-tool name; everything else (unknown / not-positively-safe / empty) → A3.\n122\tpub fn classify(tool: &str) -> Tier {\n123\t    let full = tool.to_ascii_lowercase();\n124\t    let tk = tokens(tool);\n125\t    if tk.is_empty() {\n126\t        return Tier::A3;\n127\t    }\n128\t    let has = |set: &[&str]| tk.iter().any(|t| set.contains(&t.as_str()));\n129\t    if has(A3_TOKENS) {\n130\t        return Tier::A3;\n131\t    }\n132\t    if has(A2_TOKENS) {\n133\t        return Tier::A2;\n134\t    }\n135\t    if has(A1_TOKENS) {\n136\t        return Tier::A1;\n137\t    }\n138\t    if A0_TOOLS.contains(&full.as_str()) || has(A0_VERBS) {\n139\t        return Tier::A0;\n140\t    }\n141\t    Tier::A3 // not positively classified → fail-closed to the most-gated tier\n142\t}\n143\t\n144\tpub fn gate(tier: Tier) -> Decision {\n145\t    match tier {\n146\t        Tier::A0 | Tier::A1 => Decision::Auto,\n147\t        Tier::A2 => Decision::Queued,\n148\t        Tier::A3 => Decision::ExplicitRequired,\n149\t    }\n150\t}\n151\t\n152\t#[cfg(test)]\n153\tmod tests {\n154\t    use super::*;\n155\t\n156\t    #[test]\n157\t    fn classification_matches_spec_examples() {\n158\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.search\"), Tier::A0);\n159\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"graph.query\"), Tier::A0);\n160\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.write\"), Tier::A1);\n161\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"brief.compose\"), Tier::A1);\n162\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"email.send\"), Tier::A2);\n163\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"calendar.write\"), Tier::A2); // \"calendar\" (A2) beats \"write\" (A1)\n164\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"git.push\"), Tier::A3);\n165\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"deploy.prod\"), Tier::A3);\n166\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"data.delete\"), Tier::A3);\n167\t    }\n168\t\n169\t    #[test]\n170\t    fn unknown_and_empty_are_fail_closed_a3() {\n171\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"something.unclassified\"), Tier::A3);\n172\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"\"), Tier::A3);\n173\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"weird\"), Tier::A3);\n174\t        assert_eq!(gate(classify(\"something.unclassified\")), Decision::ExplicitRequired);\n175\t    }\n176\t\n177\t    #[test]\n178\t    fn substring_collisions_do_not_lower_the_tier() {\n179\t        // the exact review findings: a dangerous op must NOT ride a benign substring down a tier\n180\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"config.overwrite\"), Tier::A3, \"overwrite != write\");\n181\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"db.overwrite\"), Tier::A3);\n182\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"files.forget\"), Tier::A3, \"forget != get; fail-closed\");\n183\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"budget.override\"), Tier::A3, \"financial + override\");\n184\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"budget.get\"), Tier::A3, \"financial target is A3 regardless of verb\");\n185\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"iam.policy.write\"), Tier::A3, \"identity write is A3, not A1\");\n186\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"firewall.rule.write\"), Tier::A3);\n187\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"transaction.sign\"), Tier::A3, \"financial signing is A3, not A1\");\n188\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"secrets.read\"), Tier::A3, \"secret read is A3, not A0\");\n189\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"credentials.get\"), Tier::A3);\n190\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"account.disable\"), Tier::A3);\n191\t    }\n192\t\n193\t    #[test]\n194\t    fn dangerous_ops_on_safe_targets_do_not_auto_run() {\n195\t        // the CRITICAL re-check finding: a target noun must NOT grant A0 to a dangerous verb\n196\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.encrypt\"), Tier::A3, \"ransomware-shaped op must NOT be A0\");\n197\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"graph.destroy\"), Tier::A3);\n198\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"entity.merge\"), Tier::A3, \"irreversible rewrite → fail-closed, not A0\");\n199\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.export\"), Tier::A2, \"full-store egress must be queued, not auto\");\n200\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"memory.dump\"), Tier::A2);\n201\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"snapshot.restore\"), Tier::A3, \"a restore reverts state → A3\");\n202\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"vault.read\"), Tier::A3, \"secret-store read is A3 regardless of verb\");\n203\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"keyring.get\"), Tier::A3);\n204\t    }\n205\t\n206\t    #[test]\n207\t    fn safe_memory_tools_still_reach_a0() {\n208\t        for t in [\n209\t            \"memory.search\", \"graph.query\", \"graph.entity\", \"episodic.range\", \"ingest.status\",\n210\t            \"threads.open\", \"commitments.due\", \"contradictions.pending\",\n211\t        ] {\n212\t            assert_eq!(classify(t), Tier::A0, \"{t} is a read-only memory tool → A0\");\n213\t        }\n214\t    }\n215\t\n216\t    #[test]\n217\t    fn danger_token_wins_over_safe_token_regardless_of_order() {\n218\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"read.then.delete\"), Tier::A3);\n219\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"delete.and.list\"), Tier::A3);\n220\t        assert_eq!(classify(\"search.and.deploy\"), Tier::A3);\n221\t    }\n222\t\n223\t    #[test]\n224\t    fn gates_are_correct() {\n225\t        assert!(gate(Tier::A0).may_run());\n226\t        assert!(gate(Tier::A1).may_run());\n227\t        assert!(!gate(Tier::A2).may_run());\n228\t        assert!(!gate(Tier::A3).may_run());\n229\t
