{"parentUuid":"d07775b1-41b8-4b98-9a40-4930ab529f0e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01N7Mafh72pG4dvn1jHENkzb","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The mesh registry (Phase 7, WS-D). Two owner-signed ledgers on the one spine:\n2\t\n3\t  • host_capability — each host advertises {os, screen, camera, gpu_vlm, always_on}, owner-signed, so\n4\t    an agent can route work to the best-capable ONLINE host from cited, tamper-evident records.\n5\t  • device authorization — a 1-of-N device ledger: the owner AUTHORIZES a phone's own Ed25519 device\n6\t    key with a signed `device_authorized` record (and `device_revoked` to remove it). The phone then\n7\t    approves A2/A3 items by signing with ITS key; `verify_approval(..., extra_pubkeys=authorized)`\n8\t    accepts it. Trust is pinned to owner-MINTED keys (RP-APPROVAL-2); the phone never holds the\n9\t    trust-root key, and can approve offline.\n10\t\n11\tAll reads are fail-closed: a descriptor/authorization that does not verify against the owner key is\n12\tignored. Reuses `governor.authn` (the same signed-event primitive kill/promotion/approval use).\"\"\"\n13\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n14\t\n15\tfrom typing import List, Optional, Set\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n18\tfrom ..agents.approvals import _approval_message\n19\tfrom ..governor.authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n20\tfrom ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n21\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex, sign\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tCAP_SIGNAL = \"mesh.host_capability\"\n25\tDEV_SIGNAL = \"mesh.device\"\n26\t_CAP_CORE = (\"signal\", \"host_id\", \"os\", \"has_screen\", \"has_camera\", \"has_gpu_vlm\", \"always_on\")\n27\t_DEV_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\", \"device_id\", \"device_pubkey\")\n28\t\n29\t\n30\t# --- host capability advertisement -----------------------------------------------------------------\n31\tdef advertise_capability(store: SpineStore, descriptor: dict, owner_key) -> int:\n32\t    core = {\"signal\": CAP_SIGNAL, \"host_id\": descriptor[\"host_id\"], \"os\": descriptor[\"os\"],\n33\t            \"has_screen\": bool(descriptor[\"has_screen\"]), \"has_camera\": bool(descriptor[\"has_camera\"]),\n34\t            \"has_gpu_vlm\": bool(descriptor[\"has_gpu_vlm\"]), \"always_on\": bool(descriptor[\"always_on\"])}\n35\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n36\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n37\t\n38\t\n39\tdef capability_map(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> dict:\n40\t    \"\"\"Latest VERIFIED capability per host_id (forged/unsigned advertisements ignored).\"\"\"\n41\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n42\t    latest: dict = {}\n43\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n44\t        p = r.payload\n45\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == CAP_SIGNAL and verify_signed(p, _CAP_CORE, tp):\n46\t            latest[p.get(\"host_id\")] = {k: p.get(k) for k in _CAP_CORE if k != \"signal\"}\n47\t    return latest\n48\t\n49\t\n50\t# --- device authorization ledger -------------------------------------------------------------------\n51\tdef authorize_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n52\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"authorized\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n53\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n54\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n55\t\n56\t\n57\tdef revoke_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n58\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"revoked\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n59\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n60\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tdef authorized_devices(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> Set[str]:\n64\t    \"\"\"The set of currently-authorized device pubkeys (owner-signed authorize, minus later revoke).\n65\t    An unsigned/forged authorization is ignored — a rogue device cannot self-authorize.\"\"\"\n66\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n67\t    state: dict = {}\n68\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n69\t        p = r.payload\n70\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == DEV_SIGNAL and p.get(\"state\") in (\"authorized\", \"revoked\") \\\n71\t                and verify_signed(p, _DEV_CORE, tp):\n72\t            state[p.get(\"device_pubkey\")] = p[\"state\"]      # latest verified state per device key\n73\t    return {pub for pub, st in state.items() if st == \"authorized\"}\n74\t\n75\t\n76\tclass DeviceApprover:\n77\t    \"\"\"Signs an approval with a DEVICE key (the phone). The record verifies via\n78\t    `verify_approval(record, owner_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(store))` — i.e. only while\n79\t    the device remains authorized. Never touches the owner trust-root key.\"\"\"\n80\t    def __init__(self, store: SpineStore, *, device_key):\n81\t        self.store = store\n82\t        self.device_key = device_key\n83\t\n84\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, approver: str, reason: str) -> int:\n85\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n86\t        sig = sign(self.device_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n87\t        payload = {\"signal\": _APPROVAL_SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq,\n88\t                   \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason, \"pubkey\": self.device_key.public_key_b64,\n89\t                   \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg), \"device\": True, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n90\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\", payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n91\t\n92\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n93\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver, reason)\n94\t\n95\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n96\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver, reason)\n97\t"}]},"uuid":"8131c2b9-d786-4648-a7ac-f4acc5eaf517","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:38.996Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d07775b1-41b8-4b98-9a40-4930ab529f0e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"c818cfdf-8c7d-45de-adc7-2e93357e3ffa","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01G4iH4asEEvaSoDh4JsthHJ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Frontier-vision EGRESS gate (Phase 7, WS-A V-ii). Sending a screen/camera frame to the frontier\n2\tAnthropic VLM uploads private bytes off the owned machine — that is A2 data-egress, not a free\n3\t\"try local then frontier\" quality bump. This gate makes the cascade doctrine-honest:\n4\t\n5\t  • The tier is DERIVED from the WARDEN oracle (`KernelClassifier` on `vision.frontier.upload` → A2),\n6\t    never self-declared.\n7\t  • Nothing uploads until a VERIFIED owner approval exists that is BOUND to this exact egress — an\n8\t    `egress_token = sha256(frame.sha256 | question)`. An approval of a different egress (a replay)\n9\t    does not match `target_seq` and does not authorize the upload.\n10\t  • Absent approval, the egress is QUEUED (an A2 proposal recorded on the spine) and the frontier\n11\t    reading is WITHHELD; the local (on-box) reading still stands.\n12\t\n13\tReuses the hardened signed `ApprovalQueue` verification (`verify_approval`) — the same fail-closed\n14\towner-key check the mesh already trusts.\"\"\"\n15\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n18\tfrom ..agents.approvals import verify_approval\n19\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n20\t\n21\tEGRESS_SIGNAL = \"vision.egress\"\n22\tFRONTIER_TOOL = \"vision.frontier.upload\"   # classified A2 by the WARDEN oracle (contains \"upload\")\n23\t\n24\t\n25\tdef egress_token(frame_sha256: str, question: str) -> str:\n26\t    \"\"\"A stable id binding an approval to exactly this (frame, question) upload.\"\"\"\n27\t    return sha256_hex(f\"{frame_sha256}|{question or ''}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tdef egress_approved(store, seq: int, token: str, trusted_pubkey) -> bool:\n31\t    \"\"\"True iff `seq` is an egress request bound to `token` AND carries a VERIFIED approval by the\n32\t    owner OR an owner-authorized device. Fail-closed: wrong token, no approval, denied, or an\n33\t    approval of a different seq → False.\"\"\"\n34\t    rec = store.get(seq)\n35\t    if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != EGRESS_SIGNAL or rec.payload.get(\"egress_token\") != token:\n36\t        return False\n37\t    from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n38\t    devices = authorized_devices(store, trusted_pubkey)\n39\t    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=seq):\n40\t        p = r.payload\n41\t        if (p.get(\"signal\") == _APPROVAL_SIGNAL and p.get(\"target_seq\") == seq\n42\t                and p.get(\"approval\") == \"approved\" and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=devices)):\n43\t            return True\n44\t    return False\n45\t"}]},"uuid":"f5bf8fd4-f1f2-4095-90cf-29f80a486aeb","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:49.361Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c818cfdf-8c7d-45de-adc7-2e93357e3ffa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

pre_mode,\n341\t                                                       \"post_hash\": None}\n342\t        return False, f\"unknown op {step.op}\", None\n343\t\n344\t    def _restore_one(self, inv: dict, *, verify_unchanged: bool) -> tuple[bool, Optional[str]]:\n345\t        \"\"\"Restore a single inverse. Returns (restored, skip_reason). `verify_unchanged` (undo) refuses\n346\t        to clobber a file the owner changed since (hash-bind), and re-resolves the path through scope.\"\"\"\n347\t        path = inv.get(\"path\")\n348\t        if verify_unchanged:\n349\t            if path is None or self.scope.resolve(path, \"read\") is None:\n350\t                return False, f\"{path}: left scope — not restored\"\n351\t            cur = Path(path)\n352\t            cur_hash = _hash_bytes(cur.read_bytes()) if cur.exists() else None\n353\t            if inv.get(\"post_hash\") is not None and cur_hash != inv.get(\"post_hash\"):\n354\t                return False, f\"{path}: changed since execute — NOT clobbered\"\n355\t            if inv.get(\"op\") == \"delete-created\" and cur_hash != inv.get(\"post_hash\"):\n356\t                return False, f\"{path}: changed since execute — not deleted\"\n357\t        try:\n358\t            if inv[\"op\"] == \"restore\":\n359\t                shutil.copyfile(inv[\"pre\"], path)\n360\t                if inv.get(\"pre_mode\") is not None:\n361\t                    os.chmod(path, inv[\"pre_mode\"])                 # BLOCK-3: restore original mode\n362\t            elif inv[\"op\"] == \"delete-created\":\n363\t                Path(path).unlink(missing_ok=True)\n364\t            else:\n365\t                return False, None                                  # irreversible — handled by caller\n366\t            return True, None\n367\t        except OSError as e:\n368\t            return False, f\"{path}: restore FAILED ({e})\"\n369\t\n370\t    def _rollback(self, applied: List[dict], *, verify_unchanged: bool = False) -> tuple[int, str]:\n371\t        n, failed, irreversible = 0, [], []\n372\t        for inv in reversed(applied):\n373\t            if inv[\"op\"] == \"irreversible\":\n374\t                irreversible.append(inv.get(\"step\"))\n375\t                continue\n376\t            ok, why = self._restore_one(inv, verify_unchanged=verify_unchanged)\n377\t            if ok:\n378\t                n += 1\n379\t            elif why:\n380\t                failed.append(why)                                  # BLOCK-5: never call a failed restore clean\n381\t        parts = []\n382\t        if failed:\n383\t            parts.append(\"PARTIAL — not restored: \" + \"; \".join(failed))\n384\t        if irreversible:\n385\t            parts.append(f\"step(s) {irreversible} IRREVERSIBLE (shell already ran)\")\n386\t        return n, (\"; \".join(parts) if parts else \"clean rollback\")\n387\t\n388\t    def undo(self, execute_seq: int) -> AgentResult:\n389\t        rec = self.store.get(execute_seq)\n390\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n391\t        if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != \"operator.execute\" or rec.payload.get(\"status\") != \"APPLIED\":\n392\t            res.notes.append(f\"seq {execute_seq} is not an applied operator execution\")\n393\t            return res\n394\t        # BLOCK-6: single-shot — refuse a second undo of the same execution.\n395\t        for r in self.store.iter_records(since_seq=execute_seq):\n396\t            if r.payload.get(\"signal\") == \"operator.undo\" and r.payload.get(\"target_seq\") == execute_seq:\n397\t                res.notes.append(f\"execute {execute_seq} was already undone (seq {r.seq}) — refusing a replay\")\n398\t                return res\n399\t        n, note = self._rollback(rec.payload.get(\"inverses\", []), verify_unchanged=True)  # hash-bound, scope-checked\n400\t        seq = self.store.append(kind=\"operation\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n401\t                                payload={\"signal\": \"operator.undo\", \"decision\": \"auto\", \"tier\": \"A1\",\n402\t                                         \"target_seq\": execute_seq, \"reverted\": n, \"note\": note,\n403\t                                         \"summary\": f\"undo of execute {execute_seq}: reverted {n} step(s) — {note}\"},\n404\t                                supersedes_id=execute_seq)\n405\t        res.applied.append(seq)\n406\t        res.notes.append(f\"undo of {execute_seq}: reverted {n} step(s) — {note}\")\n407\t        return res\n408\t"}]},"uuid":"6d7a2077-7ebf-441d-817e-d9fad9c99e94","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:49.364Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8b8672f0-5d89-4c73-8fb7-9ad62e80c10f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"a899c97b-b366-484c-ac08-38811d4e4497","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011jrKnfHghapyuuJeiprmED","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Drivers that wire the voice pipeline to real backends: `run_file` (a WAV in → response WAV out,\n2\tzero hardware needed) and `run_mic` (live full-duplex; needs a mic + speaker + optional ML).\"\"\"\n3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n4\t\n5\tfrom .pipeline import VoicePipeline\n6\t\n7\t\n8\tclass _StubAsr:\n9\t    \"\"\"Used when faster-whisper is not installed — the pipeline still runs end-to-end (segmenting\n10\t    real audio, dispatching, speaking); only the transcript is a placeholder.\"\"\"\n11\t    def transcribe(self, frames):\n12\t        return \"(no ASR model installed — run: pip install faster-whisper)\"\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tdef _make_asr(kind: str):\n16\t    if kind == \"stub\":\n17\t        return _StubAsr()\n18\t    if kind == \"elevenlabs\":\n19\t        from .backends import ElevenLabsAsr\n20\t        a = ElevenLabsAsr()\n21\t        if not a.api_key:\n22\t            print(\"  [asr] ElevenLabs key not set (ELEVENLABS_API_KEY); using stub\")\n23\t            return _StubAsr()\n24\t        return a\n25\t    try:\n26\t        from .backends import WhisperAsr\n27\t        return WhisperAsr(model=\"tiny\")\n28\t    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n29\t        print(f\"  [asr] faster-whisper unavailable ({e}); using stub\")\n30\t        return _StubAsr()\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef _make_tts(kind: str, voice: str | None):\n34\t    from .backends import SilenceTts\n35\t    if kind == \"elevenlabs\":\n36\t        from .backends import ElevenLabsTts\n37\t        t = ElevenLabsTts(voice_id=voice)   # JARVIS voice via SIGIL_TTS_VOICE_ID / --tts-voice\n38\t        if not t.api_key:\n39\t            print(\"  [tts] ElevenLabs key not set (ELEVENLABS_API_KEY); using silence fallback\")\n40\t            return SilenceTts()\n41\t        return t\n42\t    if kind == \"piper\" and voice:\n43\t        from .backends import PiperTts\n44\t        return PiperTts(voice)\n45\t    return SilenceTts()\n46\t\n47\t\n48\tdef run_file(wav_in: str, wav_out: str, *, asr: str = \"auto\", tts: str = \"silence\",\n49\t             tts_voice: str | None = None) -> VoicePipeline:\n50\t    \"\"\"Run the full pipeline over a WAV file (EnergyVad + EnergyWake + ASR + KERNEL + TTS → WAV).\n51\t    Returns the pipeline so the caller can read transcript / response / events.\"\"\"\n52\t    from .backends import EnergyVad, EnergyWake, FileAudioSource, FileSink\n53\t    from .dispatch import KernelDispatch\n54\t\n55\t    vad = EnergyVad()\n56\t    sink = FileSink(wav_out)\n57\t    p = VoicePipeline(vad, EnergyWake(vad), _make_asr(asr), _make_tts(tts, tts_voice), sink, KernelDispatch())\n58\t    p.run(FileAudioSource(wav_in).frames())\n59\t    sink.close()\n60\t    return p\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tdef run_mic(*, asr: str = \"elevenlabs\", wake: str = \"energy\", tts: str = \"elevenlabs\",\n64\t            tts_voice: str | None = None) -> None:\n65\t    \"\"\"Live full-duplex loop (needs a working mic + speaker). ASR/TTS default to ElevenLabs (the\n66\t    owner's choice); wake=openWakeWord if `wake=oww`, else the energy-onset stand-in.\"\"\"\n67\t    from .backends import EnergyVad, EnergyWake, MicAudioSource, OpenWakeWord, SpeakerSink\n68\t    from .dispatch import KernelDispatch\n69\t\n70\t    vad = EnergyVad()\n71\t    wake_c = OpenWakeWord() if wake == \"oww\" else EnergyWake(vad)\n72\t    sink = SpeakerSink()\n73\t    p = VoicePipeline(vad, wake_c, _make_asr(asr), _make_tts(tts, tts_voice), sink, KernelDispatch())\n74\t    print(\"SIGIL voice: listening (Ctrl-C to stop) …\")\n75\t    try:\n76\t        p.run(MicAudioSource().frames())\n77\t    except KeyboardInterrupt:\n78\t        pass\n79\t    finally:\n80\t        sink.close()\n81\t"}]},"uuid":"92ca0247-229d-48f7-95e3-4393619e1d11","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:30:49.366Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a899c97b-b366-484c-ac08-38811d4e4497","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

frame) -> bool:\n72\t        v = self.script[min(self.i, len(self.script) - 1)] if self.script else False\n73\t        self.i += 1\n74\t        return v\n75\t\n76\t\n77\tclass ScriptedWake:\n78\t {"parentUuid":"90aa43bb-5fe5-4371-918a-4f6e07c43e85","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01S4V51ew9zrrhiMKSeS8n32","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The SIGIL agent mesh framework (SIGIL §4/§5). Each agent has a MANDATE and an AUTONOMY\n2\tCEILING (A0–A3); everything it does is a `Proposal` carrying a tier. Proposals at or below the\n3\tauto bar (A1) and at or below the agent's ceiling are AUTO-APPLIED (written to the spine);\n4\tanything higher is QUEUED as a pending record a human must approve — never auto-executed.\n5\t\n6\tThe doctrine is enforced STRUCTURALLY, not by discipline: an agent literally cannot take an\n7\texternal-effect action, because no agent has a send/deploy/spend code path. ENVOY, in\n8\tparticular, drafts only — its ceiling is A2 and there is no method that transmits. Agent output\n9\tis provenance-linked spine records, so a brief or a draft is auditable back to what produced it.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n13\tfrom enum import IntEnum\n14\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n15\t\n16\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n17\t\n18\tAGENTS_SOURCE = \"agent\"\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tclass Tier(IntEnum):\n22\t    \"\"\"Mirror of the WARDEN tiers (SIGIL §5), ordered so `<=` compares autonomy.\"\"\"\n23\t    A0 = 0   # observe / answer\n24\t    A1 = 1   # reversible internal act (write a report/brief/event) — the AUTO bar\n25\t    A2 = 2   # external-visible / semi-reversible (send, calendar write) — QUEUED\n26\t    A3 = 3   # destructive / financial / security — explicit, never auto\n27\t\n28\t    def label(self) -> str:\n29\t        return self.name\n30\t\n31\t\n32\tAUTO_BAR = Tier.A1  # A0/A1 auto-apply; A2/A3 queue for human approval\n33\t\n34\t\n35\t@dataclass\n36\tclass Proposal:\n37\t    \"\"\"One thing an agent wants to do: emit a record (auto) or take a gated action (queued).\"\"\"\n38\t    kind: str                       # spine record kind (event/finding/brief/interaction/draft/...)\n39\t    payload: dict\n40\t    tier: Tier = Tier.A1\n41\t    parent_id: Optional[int] = None\n42\t    supersedes_id: Optional[int] = None   # this record supersedes an earlier one (e.g. a resolution)\n43\t\n44\t\n45\t@dataclass\n46\tclass AgentResult:\n47\t    agent: str\n48\t    applied: List[int] = field(default_factory=list)     # seqs written (auto)\n49\t    queued: List[dict] = field(default_factory=list)      # proposals held for approval\n50\t    notes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tclass Agent:\n54\t    \"\"\"Base agent. Subclasses implement `run()`; the base routes every Proposal through the GOVERNOR\n55\t    (SIGIL §5: kill switch, budgets, promotion — Phase 6) and records the outcome on the spine.\n56\t    Backward compatible: with no kill/cap/promotion the governor reproduces the original gate.\"\"\"\n57\t    name: str = \"AGENT\"\n58\t    mandate: str = \"\"\n59\t    ceiling: Tier = Tier.A1\n60\t\n61\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, governor=None):\n62\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n63\t        if governor is None:\n64\t            from ..governor import Governor\n65\t            governor = Governor(self.store)\n66\t        self.governor = governor\n67\t\n68\t    def run(self) -> AgentResult:\n69\t        raise NotImplementedError\n70\t\n71\t    def _dispatch(self, proposals: List[Proposal]) -> AgentResult:\n72\t        from ..governor import Outcome\n73\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n74\t        for p in proposals:\n75\t            effective = max(p.tier, Tier.A0)\n76\t            # promotion scope = the RECORD KIND (the real action written), not a self-asserted label:\n77\t            # a promotion is \"this agent may auto-approve A2 records of this kind\" (red-pen RP-3).\n78\t            decision = self.governor.decide(agent=self.name, tier=effective,\n79\t                                            ceiling=self.ceiling, scope=p.kind)\n80\t            common = {**p.payload, \"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n81\t                      \"governor\": decision.reason}\n82\t            if decision.outcome == Outcome.AUTO:\n83\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n84\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"auto\"},\n85\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n86\t                res.applied.append(seq)\n87\t            elif decision.outcome == Outcome.QUEUE:\n88\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n89\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"queued\", \"status\": \"awaiting-approval\"},\n90\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n91\t                res.queued.append({\"seq\": seq, \"kind\": p.kind, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n92\t                                   \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\") or p.payload.get(\"to\")})\n93\t            else:  # DENY — kill switch or budget; record the refusal, take no action\n94\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n95\t                                  payload={\"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n96\t                                           \"decision\": \"denied\", \"governor\": decision.reason,\n97\t                                           \"of_kind\": p.kind, \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\")},\n98\t                                  parent_id=p.parent_id)\n99\t                res.notes.append(f\"DENIED {p.kind} ({effective.label()}): {decision.reason}\")\n100\t        return res\n101\t"}]},"uuid":"f62d7386-731e-4070-bdf9-a327258b9fc2","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:31:06.949Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"90aa43bb-5fe5-4371-918a-4f6e07c43e85","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"cf0a3c57-22be-4c98-8526-90c39f373e5c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ByruEbox6aC5RWyPSM69qr","type":"tool_result","content":"=== assert_no_offense location ===\nsigil/agents/__init__.py:4:doctrine (assert_no_offense at import) — BASTION monitors own infrastructure only (Phase 5).\"\"\"\nsigil/agents/__init__.py:5:from ..reuse import assert_no_offense\nsigil/agents/__init__.py:7:assert_no_offense()\nsigil/mesh/__init__.py:5:from ..reuse import assert_no_offense\nsigil/mesh/__init__.py:7:assert_no_offense()\nsigil/mcp/server.py:16:from ..reuse import assert_no_offense\nsigil/mcp/server.py:21:assert_no_offense()  # doctrine §12: no engine module may be loaded in a SIGIL process\nsigil/reuse/__init__.py:6:`assert_no_offense()` proves it.\nsigil/reuse/__init__.py:22:    \"assert_no_offense\",\nsigil/reuse/__init__.py:26:def assert_no_offense() -> None:\nsigil/governor/__init__.py:13:doctrine (assert_no_offense).\"\"\"\nsigil/governor/__init__.py:14:from ..reuse import assert_no_offense\nsigil/governor/__init__.py:16:assert_no_offense()\nsigil/voice/__init__.py:2:full-duplex with barge-in. Offense-free by doctrine (assert_no_offense at import). Voice is just\nsigil/voice/__init__.py:5:from ..reuse import assert_no_offense\nsigil/voice/__init__.py:8:assert_no_offense()\nsigil/consolidate/__init__.py:6:zero framework.* modules (assert_no_offense enforced at import).\"\"\"\nsigil/consolidate/__init__.py:7:from ..reuse import assert_no_offense\nsigil/consolidate/__init__.py:9:assert_no_offense()  # doctrine §12: no engine module may be loaded in a SIGIL process\nsigil/perception/__init__.py:9:from ..reuse import assert_no_offense\n\n=== requirements.txt ===\ncryptography==49.0.0\nfastembed==0.8.0\nkuzu==0.11.3\nmcp==1.28.1\nonnxruntime==1.27.0\npydantic==2.13.4\nqdrant-client==1.18.0\n\n=== onnxruntime refs ===\nrequirements.txt:5:onnxruntime==1.27.0\n\n=== platform __init__ ===\n\"\"\"Per-OS backend abstraction (Phase 7, WS-D D-i). The stack above (perception, voice) speaks the\n`Frame` contract; this layer selects a `HostBackend` for the current OS at runtime, so the same\ncode runs on Linux/macOS/Windows. Linux wraps the existing capture verbatim; macOS/Windows are\nadditive; every backend returns `None` on unavailability (the honest-gap posture). Offense-free.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sys\n\nfrom ..reuse import assert_no_offense\nfrom .base import CapabilityDescriptor, HostBackend\n\nassert_no_offense()\n\n\ndef host() -> HostBackend:\n    \"\"\"The backend for this OS (selected by `sys.platform`).\"\"\"\n    if sys.platform == \"darwin\":\n        from .macos import MacOSBackend\n        return MacOSBackend()\n    if sys.platform.startswith(\"win\"):\n        from .windows import WindowsBackend\n        return WindowsBackend()\n    from .linux import LinuxBackend\n    return LinuxBackend()\n\n\n__all__ = [\"host\", \"HostBackend\", \"CapabilityDescriptor\"]\n=== macos.py ===\n\"\"\"macOS backend (Phase 7, WS-D) — screen via `screencapture`; camera left as an honest gap\n(`imagesnap` is an optional add). Additive; returns None when the native tool is absent.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nimport subprocess\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom ..perception.capture import Frame, _ocr\nfrom .base import CapabilityDescriptor, host_id, probe_always_on, probe_gpu_vlm\n\n\nclass MacOSBackend:\n    def capture_screen(self) -> Optional[Frame]:\n        if not shutil.which(\"screencapture\"):\n            return None\n        out = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".png\", prefix=\"sigil-screen-\")\n        try:\n            subprocess.run([\"screencapture\", \"-x\", out], capture_output=True, timeout=20)\n        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):\n            return None\n        if Path(out).exists() and Path(out).stat().st_size > 0:\n            return Frame.from_image(\"screen\", out, text=_ocr(out))\n        return None\n\n    def capture_camera(self) -> Optional[Frame]:\n        return None                                   # honest gap (optional imagesnap add)\n\n    def capabilities(self) -> CapabilityDescriptor:\n        return CapabilityDescriptor(\n            host_id=host_id(), os=\"macos\", has_screen=bool(shutil.which(\"screencapture\")),\n            has_camera=False, has_gpu_vlm=probe_gpu_vlm(), always_on=probe_always_on())\n=== windows.py ===\n\"\"\"Windows backend (Phase 7, WS-D) — screen via the optional `mss` package; camera an honest gap.\nAdditive; returns None when `mss` is unavailable.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom ..perception.capture import Frame, _ocr\nfrom .base import CapabilityDescriptor, host_id, probe_always_on, probe_gpu_vlm\n\n\ndef _has_mss() -> bool:\n    try:\n        import mss  # noqa: F401\n        return True\n    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n        return False\n\n\nclass WindowsBackend:\n    def capture_screen(self) -> Optional[Frame]:\n        try:\n            import mss\n            out = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".png\", prefix=\"sigil-screen-\")\n            with mss.mss() as sct:\n                sct.shot(output=out)\n            if Path(out).exists() and Path(out).stat().st_size > 0:\n                return Frame.from_image(\"screen\", out, text=_ocr(out))\n        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — no mss / grab failure → honest None\n            return None\n        return None\n\n    def capture_camera(self) -> Optional[Frame]:\n        return None\n\n    def capabilities(self) -> CapabilityDescriptor:\n        return CapabilityDescriptor(\n            host_id=host_id(), os=\"windows\", has_screen=_has_mss(), has_camera=False,\n            has_gpu_vlm=probe_gpu_vlm(), always_on=probe_always_on())","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"b445ef42-d80f-4c30-8bdd-3db0ff960da4","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:31:08.885Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cf0a3c57-22be-4c98-8526-90c39f373e5c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

WARDEN — the authorization kernel (SIGIL §5). The single choke point every tool invocation\n2\t//! crosses: classify the tool into a tier, derive the fail-closed gate decision, and (after the\n3\t//! caller executes-or-blocks) append a signed, chained action-log record. WARDEN never executes\n4\t//! tools itself; it decides and it records.\n5\t\n6\tuse std::io;\n7\tuse std::path::Path;\n8\t\n9\tuse crate::actionlog::{ActionLog, ActionRecord};\n10\tuse crate::crypto::WardenKey;\n11\tuse crate::registry::Registry;\n12\tuse crate::tiers::{gate, Decision, Tier};\n13\t\n14\tpub struct Warden {\n15\t    log: ActionLog,\n16\t    key: WardenKey,\n17\t    registry: Registry,\n18\t}\n19\t\n20\tpub struct Verdict {\n21\t    pub tier: Tier,\n22\t    pub decision: Decision,\n23\t}\n24\t\n25\timpl Warden {\n26\t    /// Open (or initialize) WARDEN under `dir`: the signing key lives in `dir/keys`, the action\n27\t    /// log at `dir/actionlog.jsonl`.\n28\t    pub fn open(dir: &Path) -> io::Result<Self> {\n29\t        let key = WardenKey::load_or_create(&dir.join(\"keys\"))?;\n30\t        let log = ActionLog::new(dir.join(\"actionlog.jsonl\"));\n31\t        let registry = Registry::load(&dir.join(\"tools.json\"));\n32\t        Ok(Warden { log, key, registry })\n33\t    }\n34\t\n35\t    /// Classify + derive the gate decision (pure — no logging, no side effects). The tier is the\n36\t    /// raise-only registry result (an explicit pin can only make a tool MORE gated than inferred).\n37\t    pub fn decide(&self, tool: &str) -> Verdict {\n38\t        let tier = self.registry.tier(tool);\n39\t        Verdict { tier, decision: gate(tier) }\n40\t    }\n41\t\n42\t    /// Append a signed action-log record for a completed (or blocked) invocation.\n43\t    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]\n44\t    pub fn record(\n45\t        &self,\n46\t        agent: &str,\n47\t        tool: &str,\n48\t        args_hash: &str,\n49\t        verdict: &Verdict,\n50\t        approver: &str,\n51\t        result_hash: &str,\n52\t        ts_epoch: u64,\n53\t    ) -> io::Result<ActionRecord> {\n54\t        self.log.append(\n55\t            &self.key, agent, tool, args_hash,\n56\t            verdict.tier.as_str(), verdict.decision.as_str(),\n57\t            approver, result_hash, ts_epoch,\n58\t        )\n59\t    }\n60\t\n61\t    pub fn verify(&self) -> Result<usize, String> {\n62\t        self.log.verify(&self.key.public_hex())\n63\t    }\n64\t\n65\t    pub fn records(&self) -> io::Result<Vec<ActionRecord>> {\n66\t        self.log.records()\n67\t    }\n68\t\n69\t    /// The on-disk signed head as (count, head_hash) — for the anti-rollback anchor.\n70\t    pub fn head(&self) -> io::Result<Option<(u64, String)>> {\n71\t        self.log.head()\n72\t    }\n73\t\n74\t    pub fn public_hex(&self) -> String {\n75\t        self.key.public_hex()\n76\t    }\n77\t\n78\t    /// Sign a message with the WARDEN key (hex) — used to authenticate anchor-set to the spine.\n79\t    pub fn sign_hex(&self, msg: &[u8]) -> String {\n80\t        self.key.sign_hex(msg)\n81\t    }\n82\t}\n83\t\n84\t#[cfg(test)]\n85\tmod tests {\n86\t    use super::*;\n87\t    use crate::crypto::sha256_hex;\n88\t\n89\t    fn tmp_dir() -> std::path::PathBuf {\n90\t        let mut p = std::env::temp_dir();\n91\t        p.push(format!(\"sigil-warden-{}\", sha256_hex(format!(\"{:?}\", std::thread::current().id()).as_bytes())));\n92\t        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&p);\n93\t        p\n94\t    }\n95\t\n96\t    #[test]\n97\t    fn a0_a1_auto_run_a2_a3_blocked() {\n98\t        let w = Warden::open(&tmp_dir()).unwrap();\n99\t        assert!(w.decide(\"memory.search\").decision.may_run(), \"A0 must auto-run\");\n100\t        assert!(w.decide(\"memory.write\").decision.may_run(), \"A1 must auto-run\");\n101\t        assert!(!w.decide(\"email.send\").decision.may_run(), \"A2 must NOT auto-run\");\n102\t        assert!(!w.decide(\"git.push\").decision.may_run(), \"A3 must NOT auto-run\");\n103\t        assert!(!w.decide(\"unknown.thing\").decision.may_run(), \"unknown must fail closed\");\n104\t    }\n105\t\n106\t    #[test]\n107\t    fn recorded_actions_verify_and_survive_reopen() {\n108\t        let dir = tmp_dir();\n109\t        {\n110\t            let w = Warden::open(&dir).unwrap();\n111\t            let v = w.decide(\"memory.search\");\n112\t            w.record(\"KERNEL\", \"memory.search\", \"b3:aa\", &v, \"auto\", \"b3:res\", 1000).unwrap();\n113\t        }\n114\t        // reopen (loads the same persisted key) and append + verify\n115\t        let w2 = Warden::open(&dir).unwrap();\n116\t        let v = w2.decide(\"email.send\");\n117\t        w2.record(\"ENVOY\", \"email.send\", \"b3:bb\", &v, \"none\", \"blocked:queued\", 1001).unwrap();\n118\t        assert_eq!(w2.verify().unwrap(), 2, \"the whole log must verify under the persisted key\");\n119\t    }\n120\t}\n121\t"}]},"uuid":"077c14c0-cf1c-4c0b-88c9-fb43444fb051","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:31:38.295Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5f43caa1-ca39-46ef-8cf9-fdc3e6e5ce05","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"f5b4f70e-4bb5-43db-8d66-65d3373f0792","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"e45de8b1-a570-4238-b570-6383504ccb4b","agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017MuJPA1cfZYNUhzsc3NeKG","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//! Per-tool tier registry (Phase 1.1) — the auditable complement to name-inference. A pin makes\n2\t//! classification EXPLICIT for a named tool. Crucially it is **RAISE-ONLY**: the effective tier\n3\t//! is `max(pin, inferred)`, so the override file (`~/.sigil/warden/tools.json`, plain text a\n4\t//! file-write attacker could edit) can only ever make a tool MORE gated, never less — it can\n5\t//! never downgrade `git.push` to auto. Legitimate de-escalation is intentionally NOT available\n6\t//! via an unsigned file (that would need a signed mechanism; a future addition).\n7\t\n8\tuse std::collections::HashMap;\n9\tuse std::path::Path;\n10\t\n11\tuse crate::tiers::{classify, Tier};\n12\t\n13\tpub struct Registry {\n14\t    pins: HashMap<String, Tier>,\n15\t}\n16\t\n17\tfn parse_tier(s: &str) -> Option<Tier> {\n18\t    match s.trim().to_ascii_uppercase().as_str() {\n19\t        \"A0\" => Some(Tier::A0),\n20\t        \"A1\" => Some(Tier::A1),\n21\t        \"A2\" => Some(Tier::A2),\n22\t        \"A3\" => Some(Tier::A3),\n23\t        _ => None,\n24\t    }\n25\t}\n26\t\n27\timpl Registry {\n28\t    pub fn empty() -> Self {\n29\t        Registry { pins: HashMap::new() }\n30\t    }\n31\t\n32\t    /// Load raise-only pins from a JSON object `{ \"tool.name\": \"A2\", ... }`. Unreadable/absent\n33\t    /// file or malformed entries are ignored (the inference engine still governs). Never fails.\n34\t    pub fn load(path: &Path) -> Self {\n35\t        let mut r = Registry::empty();\n36\t        if let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(path) {\n37\t            if let Ok(map) = serde_json::from_str::<HashMap<String, String>>(&text) {\n38\t                for (tool, tier_s) in map {\n39\t                    if let Some(t) = parse_tier(&tier_s) {\n40\t                        // case-colliding keys resolve to the HIGHER tier (deterministic +\n41\t                        // raise-only-consistent), never last-writer-wins by hash order.\n42\t                        let key = tool.to_ascii_lowercase();\n43\t                        let eff = r.pins.get(&key).map_or(t, |&e| e.max(t));\n44\t                        r.pins.insert(key, eff);\n45\t                    }\n46\t                }\n47\t            }\n48\t        }\n49\t        r\n50\t    }\n51\t\n52\t    /// The effective tier: `max(pin, inferred)`. A pin can only RAISE (never lower) the\n53\t    /// token-inference result, so the override file is not a downgrade attack surface.\n54\t    pub fn tier(&self, tool: &str) -> Tier {\n55\t        let inferred = classify(tool);\n56\t        match self.pins.get(&tool.to_ascii_lowercase()) {\n57\t            Some(&pinned) => pinned.max(inferred),\n58\t            None => inferred,\n59\t        }\n60\t    }\n61\t}\n62\t\n63\t#[cfg(test)]\n64\tmod tests {\n65\t    use super::*;\n66\t\n67\t    #[test]\n68\t    fn a_pin_can_raise_but_never_lower() {\n69\t        let mut r = Registry::empty();\n70\t        r.pins.insert(\"custom.report\".into(), Tier::A3); // owner marks a report tool extra-sensitive\n71\t        assert_eq!(r.tier(\"custom.report\"), Tier::A3, \"pin raises A1→A3\");\n72\t\n73\t        // an ATTACKER pinning a dangerous tool to A0 must NOT downgrade it\n74\t        r.pins.insert(\"git.push\".into(), Tier::A0);\n75\t        assert_eq!(r.tier(\"git.push\"), Tier::A3, \"raise-only: a pin can never lower git.push below its inferred A3\");\n76\t\n77\t        // a pin at/below the inferred tier is a no-op\n78\t        r.pins.insert(\"memory.search\".into(), Tier::A0);\n79\t        assert_eq!(r.tier(\"memory.search\"), Tier::A0);\n80\t    }\n81\t\n82\t    #[test]\n83\t    fn case_colliding_pins_resolve_to_the_higher_tier_deterministically() {\n84\t        let mut p = std::env::temp_dir();\n85\t        p.push(format!(\"sigil-reg-{}.json\", std::process::id()));\n86\t        // both keys lowercase to \"note.write\"; last-writer-wins would be nondeterministic\n87\t        std::fs::write(&p, r#\"{\"Note.Write\":\"A2\",\"note.write\":\"A1\"}\"#).unwrap();\n88\t        let r = Registry::load(&p);\n89\t        assert_eq!(r.tier(\"note.write\"), Tier::A2, \"collision → max pin (A2), raised from inferred A1\");\n90\t        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&p);\n91\t    }\n92\t\n93\t    #[test]\n94\t    fn unregistered_tools_fall_through_to_inference() {\n95\t        let r = Registry::empty();\n96\t        assert_eq!(r.tier(\"memory.search\"), Tier::A0);\n97\t        assert_eq!(r.tier(\"memory.encrypt\"), Tier::A3);\n98\t        assert_eq!(r.tier(\"totally.unknown\"), Tier::A3); // fail-closed inference\n99\t    }\n100\t}\n101\t"}]},"uuid":"c86bd616-d453-40c6-8fe5-8439a325a610","timestamp":"2026-07-18T20:31:38.491Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f5b4f70e-4bb5-43db-8d66-65d3373f0792","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"8f8ce09f-8bd2-4ee1-8146-8a4e0e1198b0","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9d49ec5c98486b2c","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAA8vPvKCpiF9NXkbCwW","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I've now read the full substrate against the real tree. Here is the design.\n\n---\n\n# SIGIL-HAND: a fast, provable, owner-armed hand-gesture control workstream\n\nA code-grounded plan. Every mechanism reuses an existing SIGIL precedent (voice FSM, vision provider seam, WARDEN oracle, mesh capability ledger, Operator gate) rather than inventing a parallel stack. Nothing here writes files; it is a build plan.\n\n## Module map (mirrors `sigil/voice/`)\n\nNew package `sigil/gesture/` shaped exactly like `sigil/voice/` (Protocols + deterministic doubles + frame-driven FSM + a live `run.py`). Streaming lives in `sigil/perception/`, injection + capability in `sigil/platform/` + `sigil/mesh/`, the authorization tier change in `kernel/src/tiers.rs`. The gesture package opens with `assert_no_offense()` like every other package `__init__` (`sigil/voice/__init__.py:8`, `sigil/mesh/__init__.py:7`).\n\n---\n\n## 1. Peak-innovative mechanisms\n\n### (a) Persistent low-latency camera STREAM\n\n**Problem in substrate:** `sigil/perception/capture.py:73 grab_camera()` cold-starts an ffmpeg process per frame (`-frames:v 1`), writes a PNG to disk, OCRs it, `timeout=20`. That is ~hundreds of ms/frame and disk-bound — unusable for control.\n\n**Design — new `sigil/perception/camera_stream.py`, mirroring `voice/backends.py:315 MicAudioSource.frames()`:**\n- `CameraStreamSource.frames()` — a generator yielding fixed-shape in-memory `numpy` frames from ONE persistent process, no per-frame disk, no OCR. Real backend spawns a single long-lived `ffmpeg -f v4l2 -i <device> -f rawvideo -pix_fmt rgb24 -s WxH -r <fps> pipe:1` and reads `W*H*3` bytes per frame off stdout in a loop (exactly the `stream.read(frame_samples)` shape of `MicAudioSource`). This is the streaming analog of the single-shot grab — reuse the v4l2/ffmpeg invocation style from `capture.grab_camera` but keep the process warm. Alternative lazy backend `cv2.VideoCapture` if OpenCV is present.\n- Frames are raw ndarrays, NOT `Frame(image_path=...)` — no sha256/PNG per frame (that cost is the reason the current grab is slow). sha256 is computed lazily only for a proof record, not on the hot path.\n- **Deterministic double `ScriptedFrameSource`** — yields a fixture list of ndarrays (or synthetic gradients), zero deps, mirrors `StaticFrame` (`capture.py:90`) and `FileAudioSource` (`backends.py:97`). This is what all FSM/classifier tests run against.\n- **Real-time posture:** drop-to-latest (process newest frame, discard backlog) so inference never queues behind a slow frame — the streaming analog of the voice pipeline consuming one frame per tick. `grab_camera` stays untouched (backward-compatible single-shot).\n\n### (b) Deep-learning from foundation — landmark model FEEDING a trained gesture classifier\n\nTwo-stage, both behind the vision-style provider seam (`perception/vision.py:27 VisionModel` Protocol with `egresses: bool`, lazy real provider + zero-dep scripted double; `consolidate/extract.py` Provider/Replay/Heuristic doubles):\n\n**Stage 1 — hand-LANDMARK model (`sigil/gesture/landmark.py`):**\n- Protocol `LandmarkModel` in `gesture/components.py`: `egresses: bool` (default `False`) + `detect(image) -> HandObservation`, where `HandObservation` = list of `Hand(landmarks: 21×3 normalized array, handedness, score)`. The `egresses` flag mirrors `VisionModel.egresses` (`vision.py:32`) so the control loop can **structurally refuse an egressing landmarker** exactly as `perceive`/`ambient_watch` refuse an egressing VLM — camera bytes can never leave via this path.\n- Real provider `OnnxHandLandmarker` — lazy-imports `onnxruntime` (**already vendored, `requirements.txt:5 onnxruntime==1.27.0`**), runs a two-graph pipeline: a palm/hand-ROI detector then a 21-keypoint landmark regressor, `InferenceSession` on CPU (or CUDA/CoreML EP if present). Preprocessing (resize/normalize) via lazy `cv2` or `numpy`. Returns honest-empty on any failure (same posture as `MoondreamVision.describe` returning `\"\"`, `vision.py:67`). The `.onnx` ships to `~/.sigil/models/hand_landmark.onnx`, checksum-pinned; absent model → honest empty, never fabricated landmarks (mirrors `capture` returning `None` not a fake frame).\n- Double `ScriptedLandmarker` — ignores the image, replays a scripted sequence of `HandObservation` (mirrors `ScriptedVad`/`ScriptedWake`, `components.py:65-90`). Every deterministic test uses this; no ML, no camera.\n\n**Stage 2 — trained GESTURE CLASSIFIER over landmark sequences (`sigil/gesture/features.py` + `classifier.py`):**\n- **Feature pipeline (`features.py`) — the \"from foundation\" core.** Per-frame, from raw 21×3 landmarks compute a **translation/scale/rotation-invariant** vector: recenter to wrist (kp0), scale by a stable span (wrist→middle-MCP), canonicalize rotation, then derive fingertip-to-palm distances, per-finger curl angles, and inter-finger spreads. Invariance is what makes it robust and is a hard, testable property (translate/scale the same hand → identical features).\n- **Static-pose head** (point / pinch / fist / open-palm / neutral): a small MLP over the per-frame feature vector.\n- **Dynamic head** (swipe L/R/U/D, circle, drag): a temporal model (1-D TCN / GRU) over a sliding WINDOW of feature vectors (the sequence). Output is per-frame `GestureReading(label, confidence, kinematics=dx/dy/velocity)`.\n- **Trained + shipped on-box.** An OFFLINE harness `sigil/gesture/train/` (NOT imported by the runtime): (1) the owner runs armed capture sessions labeling their OWN hand poses/dynamics; (2) `features.py` computes the invariant features; (3) train the MLP + TCN (tiny `torch` offline, or pure-numpy); (4) **export to ONNX** (run at inference by the vendored onnxruntime) **or** a `.npz` weight file for a pure-`numpy` forward pass (numpy is already a runtime dep) — so the classifier needs **zero new runtime deps**; (5) record model provenance/checksum on the signed spine. \"From foundation\" = our featurizer + architecture + training loop on the owner's own hand, not a vendor gesture SDK.\n- Double `ScriptedGestures` — returns a scripted `GestureReading` stream, bypassing the model. Real classifier `OnnxGestureClassifier`/`NumpyMlpClassifier` behind the same Protocol.\n\n### (c) Gesture-intent FSM with HYSTERESIS/debounce (`sigil/gesture/pipeline.py`)\n\nA direct structural copy of `voice/pipeline.py VoicePipeline`: frame-driven (`on_frame(reading)` = one tick), timing in **FRAME COUNTS not wallclock**, hysteresis so one noisy frame does nothing — reusing the exact `barge_in_frames` debounce idea (`pipeline.py:88-104`).\n\nStates: `DISARMED → ARMED_IDLE → MOVING → GESTURE_CONFIRM → (fire/queue) → COOLDOWN`, plus auto-disarm.\n\nConstructor params mirror `VoicePipeline.__init__` (`pipeline.py:32`):\n- `arm_frames` — consecutive arm-gesture frames to enter ARMED (the wake-word analog, cf. `EnergyWake.onset_frames`, `backends.py:38`).\n- `gesture_confirm_frames` — consecutive identical high-confidence frames to FIRE a discrete action (the `barge_in_frames` hysteresis: a single misclassified frame cannot fire a click).\n- `margin_min` / `conf_min` — if top1−top2 confidence margin is small OR confidence is low → treated as **neutral**, counter resets → **NOTHING fires** (fail-safe on ambiguity).\n- `hand_lost_frames` — consecutive no-hand frames → **auto-disarm** (fail-safe: hand leaves frame ⇒ control stops).\n- `session_max_frames` — absolute session cap → auto-disarm (mirrors `max_utterance_frames` so a session can never wedge, `pipeline.py:75`).\n- `cooldown_frames` — after a discrete fire, ignore new discrete gestures (repeat-debounce).\n- `move_deadzone` + a **One-Euro filter** on the pointer position — adaptive low-lag jitter smoothing (low jitter at rest, no lag on fast motion), the advanced alternative to naive averaging. This is the \"cursor feel\" innovation and adds no wallclock latency.\n\nOutput is a `GestureIntent` (a `move dx/dy`, or a discrete `click/scroll/type/drag/launch`), NOT a raw injection — the FSM decides intent; authorization is a separate layer (below). This matches the voice split: the pipeline produces text/intent, `dispatch` crosses the WARDEN gate (`voice/dispatch.py`).\n\n### (d) KEYSTONE SECURITY — owner-armed session + deliberate WARDEN oracle extension\n\n**Two enforcement layers so a gesture can NEVER trigger an unintended A2/A3 action.**\n\n**Layer 1 — the owner-armed SESSION (`sigil/gesture/session.py`).** Opt-in, indicator-lit, spine-logged — modeled on the ambient-vision opt-in and the frontier-egress gate (`perception/egress.py`):\n- Arming appends a signed `gesture.session_armed` record to the append-only spine (the proof-of-authorization; reuse `governor.authn.signed_payload` / mesh `advertise_capability` signing style, `mesh/registry.py:31`). Disarm appends `gesture.session_disarmed`. The indicator is lit whenever armed and cleared on disarm/crash in a `finally` block (mirror `SpeakerSink.close()` in `voice/run.py:79`).\n- A `session_id` bound to the signed arm record + a monotonic nonce is the ONLY token that authorizes injection. Bounded lifetime (`session_max_frames` / wallclock cap). A stale/replayed token is rejected.\n- **The session is the owner's blanket consent to REVERSIBLE pointer movement + click within its bounds** — nothing more. Arming grants the bounded auto-tier for `pointer.move`/`click`/`scroll`; it does NOT grant `type`/`launch`.\n\n**Layer 2 — every discrete action's tier is DERIVED from the WARDEN oracle and gated** (reuse `KernelClassifier.classify`, `agents/kernel_classify.py:28`, which shells the fail-closed Rust `classify`, and the Operator's derive-tier→preview→approval-gate→execute pattern, `operator.py:259-263`). The `SessionGate` maps `GestureIntent → tool token → KernelClassifier → gate`:\n- A1 (`move`/`click`/`scroll`) → auto, **but only while a valid session token exists** (injected).\n- A2 (`type`/`key-combo`/`app-launch`) → **QUEUED**: the gate emits a signed A2 proposal and **withholds the keystrokes** until a verified owner/device approval bound to the exact action lands — reusing `egress_approved`'s token-binding (`egress.py:30`, `token = sha256(frame|question)`; here `token = sha256(session_id | tool | args_digest)`) and `operator._approved` / `authorized_devices` / `DeviceApprover` (`mesh/registry.py:76`, phone approves by signing). A gesture alone can therefore **never type a password or launch an app** — those always require a deliberate approval.\n- A3 or unknown → refused, logged (fail-closed).\n\n**The deliberate `kernel/src/tiers.rs` extension (security-critical).** Today every input token fails closed to A3 (`tiers.rs:122` — `input.click`, `pointer.move`, `input.type` are in no allowlist → A3). We add input authorization **as an EXACT-NAME table, not tokens** — mirroring the existing `A0_TOOLS` exact-name allowlist (`tiers.rs:107`) rather than the token sets, because exact names have **zero collision risk** with real tools:\n- New `INPUT_A1` (exact names): `hid.pointer.move`, `hid.pointer.click`, `hid.pointer.scroll`, `hid.pointer.drag` → A1 (reversible; session-boundedness enforced in Layer 1).\n- New `INPUT_A2` (exact names): `hid.key.type`, `hid.key.combo`, `hid.app.launch` → A2 (keystroke/shortcut/exec-adjacent → queued).\n- Checked in `classify()` **AFTER the danger-first A3/A2/A1 token checks** (`tiers.rs:129-137`) and before A0 fallback, so **danger always wins**: `hid.pointer.delete` still hits the `delete` A3 token → A3. Everything else under `hid.*` or any unknown input name stays fail-closed A3. Because the raise-only registry (`registry.rs:54 max(pin, inferred)`) sits on top, the owner can only ever make an input tool MORE gated, never less.\n\nThis guarantees the requirement: bare tokens like `move`/`type` are **never** added to the token sets (a `file.move` or `data.type` must NOT become auto), and the only path to A1 injection is an exact compiled `hid.*` name that carries no danger token.\n\n### (e) Per-OS injection backends + camera streaming, slotted into HostBackend + signed capability flags\n\nExtend `sigil/platform/base.py`:\n- New Protocol `InputBackend`: `move(dx,dy)`, `move_to(x,y)`, `click(button)`, `scroll(dx,dy)`, `type(text)`, `key_combo(keys)`, `capabilities()`. All lazy-imported behind the provider (heavy deps stay out of `requirements.txt`, per the voice ML convention).\n- `LinuxInputBackend` — prefer kernel `uinput` (lazy `python-uinput`, works under Wayland) → `ydotool` daemon → X11 `xdotool`/XTEST; honest-None if none present (same degrade posture as `LinuxBackend.capture_camera`, `platform/linux.py:19`).\n- `MacOSInputBackend` — Quartz `CGEventCreateMouseEvent`/`CGEventCreateKeyboardEvent`/`CGEventPost` via lazy `pyobjc`; requires Accessibility permission (documented honest gap, like the macOS camera gap `platform/macos.py:29`).\n- `WindowsInputBackend` — `SendInput` via **`ctypes` (stdlib — no new dep)**, or lazy `pynput`.\n- Double `RecordingInputBackend` — records calls, never touches the OS (mirrors `BufferSink`, `components.py:121`). Every test drives this.\n- `platform/__init__.py` gains `input_backend()` selecting by `sys.platform` (mirror `host()`, `platform/__init__.py`).\n\nSigned capability flags — add `has_hid_inject` and `has_camera_stream` to `CapabilityDescriptor` (`platform/base.py:14`), `to_dict`, and — because capabilities are OWNER-SIGNED — to the signed core `_CAP_CORE` (`mesh/registry.py:26`) plus `advertise_capability`'s core dict (`registry.py:31`). `capability_map` picks them up automatically via the `_CAP_CORE` iteration (`registry.py:46`). Each OS backend's `capabilities()` reports the flags (uinput/ydotool present? stream device present?).\n\n---\n\n## 2. Test + real-proof strategy per slice + red-pen lenses\n\n**Deterministic doubles everywhere; a real proof gated skip-if-absent** (mirror the voice tests that run the whole pipeline on scripted frames with no hardware, `tests/test_voice.py`).\n\n- **S0** — drive `GesturePipeline.on_frame` with `ScriptedGestures`: prove (i) a single noisy misclassified frame fires NOTHING (needs `gesture_confirm_frames` consecutive); (ii) low-margin/low-confidence → neutral → nothing; (iii) `hand_lost_frames` of no-hand auto-disarms; (iv) `session_max_frames` hard-stops; (v) the FSM adds ZERO buffering latency — an intent fires **exactly** `gesture_confirm_frames` after onset (frame-count invariant, deterministic). *Real proof:* none needed (pure logic).\n- **S1** — Rust tests mirroring `tiers.rs:156-229`: `hid.pointer.move`→A1, `hid.key.type`→A2, `hid.app.launch`→A2, `hid.pointer.delete`→A3 (danger wins), unknown `hid.foo`→A3, plus a Python test through `KernelClassifier`. Re-run the ENTIRE existing tiers suite unchanged (must pass) + assert non-input tools (`file.move`, `data.type`, `content.type`, `entity.merge`) are **unaffected**.\n- **S2** — `ScriptedFrameSource` yields deterministic ndarrays. *Real proof:* capture N frames off `/dev/video0` via the persistent ffmpeg pipe, assert shape/FPS; skip if `not Path(\"/dev/video0\").exists()` or ffmpeg absent (same guard as `grab_camera`, `capture.py:76`).\n- **S3** — `ScriptedLandmarker` for all logic. *Real proof:* run `OnnxHandLandmarker` on a fixture hand image, assert 21 keypoints; skip-if-model-absent + skip-if-onnxruntime-EP-absent.\n- **S4** — featurizer invariance tests (translate+scale a landmark set → identical features), classifier double round-trips. *Real proof:* load the exported ONNX/npz, classify a labeled fixture window; skip-if-weights-absent.\n- **S5** — `RecordingInputBackend` records `move/click/type`. Indicator lit-on-arm / cleared-in-`finally`.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Doctrine-safety analysis + abuse vectors\n\n**Doctrine alignment**\n- **Offense-free:** `assert_no_offense()` at `gesture/__init__` (no `framework.*` loads, `reuse/__init__.py:26`); on-box models only; `LandmarkModel.egresses=False` and the control loop structurally refuses any egressing model (mirror `perceive`); no network on the control path. The system observes the owner's OWN hand and controls the owner's OWN device — there is no remote target, no scanning, no injection into anything but the local session.\n- **Fail-closed:** unknown/ambiguous gesture → nothing; hand lost → disarm; oracle error → A3 (`kernel_classify.py:42`); missing backend/model → honest `None`/empty (never fabricated); session expired → stop.\n- **Owner-armed:** explicit opt-in, indicator-lit, spine-logged, bounded-lifetime session; arming is the recorded consent.\n- **WARDEN-gated input:** every discrete action tier-derived from the same fail-closed oracle; A2+ queues for owner/device approval; raise-only registry can only tighten.\n- **Local-first / prove-don't-guess:** everything on-box; the signed append-only spine carries proof-of-authorization for the session AND each gated action; deterministic doubles + real proofs.\n\n**Abuse vectors a reviewer will hunt**\n- *Covert surveillance via the camera* — mitigated: opt-in, indicator-lit, spine-logged; frames in-memory only (no PNG persistence, unlike `grab_camera`); only landmarks flow downstream; on-box landmarker; no frame ever reaches a frontier model on this path (structural refusal).\n- *Bystander's hand in frame* controlling the device — single-hand, owner-armed, deliberate arm-gesture; hand-identity binding is a documented honest gap.\n- *Injection into a privileged window* (UAC/sudo/lock-screen/password field) — `type`/`combo` are A2 (queued), so a credential-entry gesture cannot auto-fire; recommend best-effort refusal on a known secure desktop (honest gap). Injection inherits only the owner's own permission context.\n- *Session token theft/replay* — token bound to the signed arm record + nonce + monotonic; replay rejected.\n- *TOCTOU on an A2 approval* — bind approval to the action digest (reuse `egress_token` + Operator anti-TOCTOU re-derive, `operator.py:252-260`).\n- *Signed-core migration* — adding `has_hid_inject` to `_CAP_CORE` invalidates OLD advertisements under the new core tuple; hosts re-advertise at startup, so this is acceptable but must be called out (and old-record handling defaulted to `False`).\n- *Action-log flooding* — per-move WARDEN records at 30 FPS would DoS the spine; design movement as session-scoped telemetry (only the arm/disarm + discrete actions are logged), exactly as voice logs the interaction not each audio frame.\n- *Click firing a destructive UI button* — residual: WARDEN classifies the tool, not the UI target. Mitigated by session-bounded + indicator + owner-present + reversible-at-input; a \"protected-region / dwell-confirm\" mode is a documented future tightening.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Speed / latency design and how \"fast\" is proven\n\n**Targets (per frame, camera-photon → cursor-move):** budget < one 30-FPS frame (~33 ms), stretch 60 FPS (~16 ms). Stage budget:\n\n| Stage | Target |\n|---|---|\n| stream read (persistent rawvideo pipe, no cold ffmpeg, no disk) | 2–5 ms |\n| preprocess/resize/normalize | 2–3 ms |\n| landmark ONNX (palm+21kp, onnxruntime CPU; less on GPU/CoreML EP) | 10–20 ms CPU |\n| featurize + classify (numpy MLP / small ONNX) | ~1 ms |\n| FSM tick + One-Euro smoothing | < 0.1 ms |\n| injection (uinput/CGEvent/SendInput) | < 1 ms |\n\n**Design levers for \"fast\":** persistent stream (kills the `grab_camera` cold-start + disk write, the single biggest win); drop-to-latest so inference never queues behind a slow frame; FSM timing in **frame counts not wallclock** (as voice, `pipeline.py`), so the debounce cost is a known, bounded number of frames not an arbitrary sleep; One-Euro filter for low-lag smoothing (no averaging lag); classifier as an on-box numpy/ONNX forward pass (no new heavy dep); optional CUDA/CoreML EP auto-selected when present.\n\n**How \"fast\" is PROVEN (honestly):**\n- A per-stage timing bench over N real frames reports measured p50/p95 ms per stage, printed and (optionally) spine-recorded; **skip-if-absent** rather than fabricate; never claims GPU numbers on a CPU box.\n- A deterministic FSM invariant test proves the pipeline adds **zero** buffering latency beyond the configured `gesture_confirm_frames` — an intent fires at exactly onset + confirm frames — so end-to-end latency is (stage sum) + (confirm-frame debounce), both known quantities.\n- A drop-to-latest queue-depth assertion proves the loop stays real-time (backlog does not grow) under a slow-inference injection.\n\n---\n\n### Critical Files for Implementation\n- /home/kali/sigil/kernel/src/tiers.rs — the security keystone: add `INPUT_A1`/`INPUT_A2` exact-name tables + danger-first `classify` branch (S1).\n- /home/kali/sigil/sigil/gesture/pipeline.py — new hysteresis/debounce FSM, structural copy of `voice/pipeline.py` (S0).\n- /home/kali/sigil/sigil/gesture/session.py — new owner-armed session + injection gate (tier-derive → gate → inject-or-queue);
